id,english,transliteration,nepalbhasa_newari
"0","I wake up early.","ji nhāpāṃ danā .","जि न्हापां दना ।"
"1","You wake up late.","cha libākka dana .","छ लिबाक्क दन ।"
"2","He wakes up at six.","va khutāḥ ilay danī .","व खुताः इलय् दनी ।"
"3","She wakes up at seven.","va nhay tāḥ ilay danī .","व न्हय् ताः इलय् दनी ।"
"4","We wake up together.","jhī nāpaṃ dane .","झी नापं दने ।"
"5","They wake up at dawn.","ipiṃ sutha nhāpāṃ danī .","इपिं सुथ न्हापां दनी ।"
"6","I brush my teeth every morning.","jiṃ nhyābaleṃ suthay jigu vā bule .","जिं न्ह्याबलें सुथय् जिगु वा बुले ।"
"7","She brushes her teeth after breakfast.","vaṃ kaulā yāyedhuṃkāḥ vā bula .","वं कौला यायेधुंकाः वा बुल ।"
"8","He takes a shower before work.","vaṃ jyā yāye nhyaḥ mvaḥ lhui .","वं ज्या याये न्ह्यः म्वः ल्हुइ ।"
"9","We eat breakfast together.","jipiṃ nāpaṃ kaulā yānā .","जिपिं नापं कौला याना ।"
"10","I go to work every day.","ji nhiṃnhiṃ jyāy vane .","जि न्हिंन्हिं ज्याय् वने ।"
"11","She goes to school by bus.","va basaṃ bvanekuthii vanī .","व बसं ब्वनेकुथिइ वनी ।"
"12","He walks to the office.","va jyākuthii nyāsi vana .","व ज्याकुथिइ न्यासि वन ।"
"13","They drive to work.","imisaṃ jyā yāyeta ḍrāibha yāi .","इमिसं ज्या यायेत ड्राइभ याइ ।"
"14","We take the train to the city.","jipiṃ śaharay vanegu ṭrenaṃ vane .","जिपिं शहरय् वनेगु ट्रेनं वने ।"
"15","I come home at five.","ji nyātāḥ ilay cheṃy vayā .","जि न्याताः इलय् छेँय् वया ।"
"16","She comes home late.","va libākka cheṃy vai .","व लिबाक्क छेँय् वइ ।"
"17","He arrives at the office at eight.","va cyātāḥ ilay jyākuthii thyanī .","व च्याताः इलय् ज्याकुथिइ थ्यनी ।"
"18","I leave the house at seven.","ji nhay tāḥ ilay cheṃnaṃ pihāṃ vane .","जि न्हय् ताः इलय् छेँनं पिहां वने ।"
"19","We leave for work together.","jipiṃ nāpaṃ jyāyā niṃtiṃ pihāṃ vane .","जिपिं नापं ज्याया निंतिं पिहां वने ।"
"20","I do not wake up early.","ji nhāpāṃ dane makhu .","जि न्हापां दने मखु ।"
"21","She does not take the bus.","vaṃ basa makāla .","वं बस मकाल ।"
"22","He does not eat breakfast.","vaṃ kaulā mayāḥ .","वं कौला मयाः ।"
"23","We do not drive to work.","jipiṃ jyāy vaneta gāḍī caleyāye makhu .","जिपिं ज्याय् वनेत गाडी चलेयाये मखु ।"
"24","They do not walk to school.","ipiṃ bvanekuthii nyāsi vanī makhu .","इपिं ब्वनेकुथिइ न्यासि वनी मखु ।"
"25","Do you wake up early?","cha nhāpāṃ danā lā ?","छ न्हापां दना ला ?"
"26","Does he take a shower?","vaṃ mvaḥ lhui lā ?","वं म्वः ल्हुइ ला ?"
"27","Does she eat breakfast?","vaṃ kaulā yāta lā ?","वं कौला यात ला ?"
"28","What time do you wake up?","cha guli ilay danegu ?","छ गुलि इलय् दनेगु ?"
"29","Where do you work?","chaṃ gana jyā yānā ?","छं गन ज्या याना ?"
"30","How do you get to work?","cha jyāy gathe yānāḥ vanegu ?","छ ज्याय् गथे यानाः वनेगु ?"
"31","When does he come home?","va gabale cheṃy vai ?","व गबले छेँय् वइ ?"
"32","Why do they leave early?","ipiṃ chāy nhāpāṃ vana ?","इपिं छाय् न्हापां वन ?"
"33","I woke up early yesterday.","ji mhigaḥ nhāpāṃ danā .","जि म्हिगः न्हापां दना ।"
"34","She ate breakfast this morning.","vaṃ thauṃ suthay kaulā yāta .","वं थौं सुथय् कौला यात ।"
"35","He walked to work yesterday.","va mhigaḥ jyā yāyeta nyāsi vana .","व म्हिगः ज्या यायेत न्यासि वन ।"
"36","We took the bus last week.","jimisaṃ vaṃgu vālay basa kayā .","जिमिसं वंगु वालय् बस कया ।"
"37","They drove to the city.","ipiṃ śaharay moṭara cale yāta .","इपिं शहरय् मोटर चले यात ।"
"38","I did not eat breakfast.","jiṃ kaulā mayānā .","जिं कौला मयाना ।"
"39","She did not go to work.","va jyāy mavaṃ .","व ज्याय् मवं ।"
"40","Did you wake up early?","cha nhāpāṃ danā lā ?","छ न्हापां दना ला ?"
"41","Did he come home late?","va libākka cheṃy vala lā ?","व लिबाक्क छेँय् वल ला ?"
"42","What did you eat for breakfast?","chaṃ kaulāy chu nayā ?","छं कौलाय् छु नया ?"
"43","I will wake up early tomorrow.","ji kanhay nhāpāṃ dane .","जि कन्हय् न्हापां दने ।"
"44","She will take the bus.","vaṃ basa kāi .","वं बस काइ ।"
"45","He will walk to work.","va jyā yāyeta nyāsi vanī .","व ज्या यायेत न्यासि वनी ।"
"46","We will eat dinner together.","jhī nāpaṃ beli yāye .","झी नापं बेलि याये ।"
"47","They will come home late.","ipiṃ cheṃy libākka vai .","इपिं छेँय् लिबाक्क वइ ।"
"48","I will not go to work tomorrow.","ji kanhay jyāy vanemakhu .","जि कन्हय् ज्याय् वनेमखु ।"
"49","Will you eat breakfast?","chaṃ kaulā yāyegu lā ?","छं कौला यायेगु ला ?"
"50","When will she come home?","va gabale cheṃy vai ?","व गबले छेँय् वइ ?"
"51","I am eating breakfast.","kaulā yānācvanā .","कौला यानाच्वना ।"
"52","She is walking to school.","va bvanekuthii nyāsi vanācvana .","व ब्वनेकुथिइ न्यासि वनाच्वन ।"
"53","He is taking a shower.","vaṃ mvaḥ lhuyācvana .","वं म्वः ल्हुयाच्वन ।"
"54","We are getting ready.","jipiṃ tayāra juyācvanā .","जिपिं तयार जुयाच्वना ।"
"55","They are leaving now.","ipiṃ āḥ vanetyaṃgu du .","इपिं आः वनेत्यंगु दु ।"
"56","I was eating when you called.","chaṃ saḥtūbalay ji nayācvanāgu khaḥ .","छं सःतूबलय् जि नयाच्वनागु खः ।"
"57","She was sleeping when I arrived.","ji thyaṃbalay va dyanācvana .","जि थ्यंबलय् व द्यनाच्वन ।"
"58","I have already eaten.","naye dhuna .","नये धुन ।"
"59","She has already left.","va vanedhuṃkala .","व वनेधुंकल ।"
"60","He has not eaten yet.","vaṃ āḥtaka naye makhaṃni .","वं आःतक नये मखंनि ।"
"61","We have been here since morning.","jipiṃ suthaṃniseṃ thana du .","जिपिं सुथंनिसें थन दु ।"
"62","They had already left when I arrived.","ji thyaṃbalay ipiṃ vanedhuṃkūgu khaḥ .","जि थ्यंबलय् इपिं वनेधुंकूगु खः ।"
"63","Wake up!","danigu!","दनिगु!"
"64","Do not be late!","libāye mate !","लिबाये मते !"
"65","Eat your breakfast.","chaṃgu kaulā na .","छंगु कौला न ।"
"66","Go to bed early.","nhāpāṃ dyaṃ .","न्हापां द्यँ ।"
"67","Let us leave now.","āḥ jhīpiṃ vanenu .","आः झीपिं वनेनु ।"
"68","I can wake up early.","ji nhāpāṃ danephu .","जि न्हापां दनेफु ।"
"69","She must go to work.","va jyāy vane he māḥ .","व ज्याय् वने हे माः ।"
"70","He should eat breakfast.","vaṃ suthasiyā kaulā nayemāḥ .","वं सुथसिया कौला नयेमाः ।"
"71","We need to leave now.","jhī āḥ he tvaḥtemāḥgu du .","झी आः हे त्वःतेमाःगु दु ।"
"72","You may stay home today.","cha thauṃ cheṃy he cvanephu .","छ थौं छेँय् हे च्वनेफु ।"
"73","Can you help me?","chaṃ jita: gvāhāli yāye phai lā ?","छं जित: ग्वाहालि याये फै ला ?"
"74","I cannot find my keys.","jiṃ jigu tāḥcā luike maphuta .","जिं जिगु ताःचा लुइके मफुत ।"
"75","Breakfast is prepared by my mother.","kaulā jimi māmaṃ dayekī .","कौला जिमि मामं दयेकी ।"
"76","The house was cleaned yesterday.","mhigaḥ cheṃ sucukucu yāḥgu khaḥ .","म्हिगः छेँ सुचुकुचु याःगु खः ।"
"77","Dinner will be cooked by him.","beli vaṃ he dayekī .","बेलि वं हे दयेकी ।"
"78","If I wake up early, I will exercise.","ji nhāpāṃ dana dhāḥsā, jiṃ byāyāma yāye .","जि न्हापां दन धाःसा, जिं ब्यायाम याये ।"
"79","If she had time, she would cook breakfast.","vayāke ī dusā, vaṃ kaulā dayekī .","वयाके ई दुसा, वं कौला दयेकी ।"
"80","If I had woken up earlier, I would not have been late.","ji nhāpā he danāgu jūsā ji libāigu makhu .","जि न्हापा हे दनागु जूसा जि लिबाइगु मखु ।"
"81","I wake up and I eat breakfast.","ji danā ale jiṃ kaulā yānā .","जि दना अले जिं कौला याना ।"
"82","He works but she studies.","vaṃ jyā yāi tara vaṃ bvanī .","वं ज्या याइ तर वं ब्वनी ।"
"83","I eat breakfast because I am hungry.","jitaḥ naye pityāḥguliṃ jiṃ kaulā yānā .","जितः नये पित्याःगुलिं जिं कौला याना ।"
"84","She brushes her teeth before she leaves.","vaṃ vane nhyaḥ vā bula .","वं वने न्ह्यः वा बुल ।"
"85","Although I am tired, I go to work.","tyānulasāṃ ji jyāy vanā .","त्यानुलसां जि ज्याय् वना ।"
"86","This is my house.","thva jigu cheṃ khaḥ .","थ्व जिगु छेँ खः ।"
"87","That is her car.","va vayāgu kāra khaḥ .","व वयागु कार खः ।"
"88","I dress myself.","jiṃ thaḥthamhaṃ vasaḥ puṃ .","जिं थःथम्हं वसः पुं ।"
"89","He shaves himself every morning.","vaṃ nhyābaleṃ suthay thaḥthamhaṃ dāri dāri tvapui .","वं न्ह्याबलें सुथय् थःथम्हं दारि दारि त्वपुइ ।"
"90","I wake up earlier than my brother.","ji jimi kijā svayāṃ nhyaḥ he danā .","जि जिमि किजा स्वयां न्ह्यः हे दना ।"
"91","She is the fastest runner in our family.","jimi cheṃjalay va dakalay yākanaṃ bvāṃy vanīmha khaḥ .","जिमि छेँजलय् व दकलय् याकनं ब्वाँय् वनीम्ह खः ।"
"92","Today is busier than yesterday.","mhigaḥ svayāṃ thauṃ apvaḥ limalāḥ .","म्हिगः स्वयां थौं अप्वः लिमलाः ।"
"93","There is a park near my house.","jigu cheṃ likka pārka du .","जिगु छेँ लिक्क पार्क दु ।"
"94","There are many people on the street.","satakay yakva manūta du .","सतकय् यक्व मनूत दु ।"
"95","Everyone goes to work.","sakaleṃ jyāy vanī .","सकलें ज्याय् वनी ।"
"96","Nobody stays home during the week.","vālay suṃ naṃ cheṃy macvanī .","वालय् सुं नं छेँय् मच्वनी ।"
"97","Some people wake up very early.","guliṃ manūta tasakaṃ nhāpāṃ danī .","गुलिं मनूत तसकं न्हापां दनी ।"
"98","The man who lives next door is a teacher.","likkasaṃ cvaṃmha manū guru khaḥ .","लिक्कसं च्वंम्ह मनू गुरु खः ।"
"99","The bus that I take is always late.","jiṃ gayāgu basa nhyābaleṃ libāi .","जिं गयागु बस न्ह्याबलें लिबाइ ।"
"100","The house where I grew up is very old.","ji bvalaṃgu cheṃ tasakaṃ pulāṃ .","जि ब्वलंगु छेँ तसकं पुलां ।"
"101","I always eat lunch at noon.","jiṃ nhyābaleṃ nhinay lanca nayegu yānā .","जिं न्ह्याबलें न्हिनय् लन्च नयेगु याना ।"
"102","She sometimes walks to work.","va gabaleṃ gabaleṃ jyā yāyeta nyāsi vanī .","व गबलें गबलें ज्या यायेत न्यासि वनी ।"
"103","He never eats dinner late.","vaṃ gubaleṃ libākka beli mayāḥ .","वं गुबलें लिबाक्क बेलि मयाः ।"
"104","We usually go to bed at ten.","jipiṃ apvaḥ dhayā theṃ jhitāḥ ilay dyanegu .","जिपिं अप्वः धया थें झिताः इलय् द्यनेगु ।"
"105","I brush my teeth twice a day.","jiṃ nhiṃ nikvaḥ vā bule .","जिं न्हिं निक्वः वा बुले ।"
"106","The keys are on the table.","tāḥcāta ṭebulay du .","ताःचात टेबुलय् दु ।"
"107","My phone is in my bag.","jigu phona jigu bāy gay du .","जिगु फोन जिगु बाय् गय् दु ।"
"108","The car is in front of the house.","gāḍī cheṃyā nhyaḥne du .","गाडी छेँया न्ह्यःने दु ।"
"109","The park is behind the school.","bvanekuthiyā lyūne pārka du .","ब्वनेकुथिया ल्यूने पार्क दु ।"
"110","I sit next to my colleague.","ji jimi pāsāyā likka cvanā .","जि जिमि पासाया लिक्क च्वना ।"
"111","He said that he was tired.","vaṃ dhāla ki va tyānula .","वं धाल कि व त्यानुल ।"
"112","She told me that she would be late.","vaṃ jitaḥ dhāla ki va libāi .","वं जितः धाल कि व लिबाइ ।"
"113","They asked if I was coming.","ji vayācvanā lā dhakāḥ nyana .","जि वयाच्वना ला धकाः न्यन ।"
"114","I clean the house on Saturdays.","jiṃ sanibārakhunhu cheṃ sucukucu yāye .","जिं सनिबारखुन्हु छेँ सुचुकुचु याये ।"
"115","She does the laundry every week.","vaṃ vāḥpatikaṃ vasaḥ hiigu jyā yāi .","वं वाःपतिकं वसः हिइगु ज्या याइ ।"
"116","He cooks dinner for the family.","vaṃ cheṃjaḥyā niṃtiṃ beli dayekī .","वं छेँजःया निंतिं बेलि दयेकी ।"
"117","We watch television in the evening.","jipiṃ saniilay ṭelibhijana svayā .","जिपिं सनिइलय् टेलिभिजन स्वया ।"
"118","They play in the garden after school.","ipiṃ bvanekuthi sidhayekāḥ kyabay mhitī .","इपिं ब्वनेकुथि सिधयेकाः क्यबय् म्हिती ।"
"119","I read a book before bed.","jiṃ dyane nhyaḥ saphū bvanā .","जिं द्यने न्ह्यः सफू ब्वना ।"
"120","She listens to music while cooking.","vaṃ nasā jvare yāibalay saṃgīta nyanī .","वं नसा ज्वरे याइबलय् संगीत न्यनी ।"
"121","He takes the dog for a walk.","vaṃ khicāyāta cāḥhike yaṃkala .","वं खिचायात चाःहिके यंकल ।"
"122","I drink coffee every morning.","jiṃ nhyābaleṃ suthay kaphī tvanā .","जिं न्ह्याबलें सुथय् कफी त्वना ।"
"123","The children play outside.","masta pine mhitī .","मस्त पिने म्हिती ।"
"124","My mother cooks the best food.","jimi māmaṃ dakalay bāṃlāḥgu nasā jvare yāi .","जिमि मामं दकलय् बांलाःगु नसा ज्वरे याइ ।"
"125","Our neighbor is very friendly.","jimi jaḥlākhaḥlā tasakaṃ milanasāra .","जिमि जःलाखःला तसकं मिलनसार ।"
"126","I need to buy groceries.","jitaḥ kirānā sāmāna nyāyemāḥgu du .","जितः किराना सामान न्यायेमाःगु दु ।"
"127","She forgot her umbrella.","vaṃ vayāgu kusā lvaḥmaṃkala .","वं वयागु कुसा ल्वःमंकल ।"
"128","The alarm clock rings at six.","alārma ghaḍī khutāḥ ilay nyāi .","अलार्म घडी खुताः इलय् न्याइ ।"
"129","I usually take a nap after lunch.","jiṃ apvaḥ dhayā theṃ lanca dhuṃkāḥ bhacā nhyalaṃ cāyekā .","जिं अप्वः धया थें लन्च धुंकाः भचा न्ह्यलं चायेका ।"
"130","He is getting dressed.","vaṃ vasaḥ punācvana .","वं वसः पुनाच्वन ।"
"131","I have been living here for five years.","ji thana cvanāgu nyādaṃ dayedhuṃkala .","जि थन च्वनागु न्यादँ दयेधुंकल ।"
"132","She has just arrived.","va nakatini thyaṃkaḥ vala .","व नकतिनि थ्यंकः वल ।"
"133","Where are my shoes?","jigu lākāṃ gana du ?","जिगु लाकां गन दु ?"
"134","How many rooms does your house have?","chigu cheṃy gvaḥkū kvathā du ?","छिगु छेँय् ग्वःकू क्वथा दु ?"
"135","I wish I could sleep more.","ji apvaḥ dyane phaḥsā jyū .","जि अप्वः द्यने फःसा ज्यू ।"
"136","If it rains, I will stay home.","vā vala dhāḥsā ji cheṃy he cvane .","वा वल धाःसा जि छेँय् हे च्वने ।"
"137","Let me help you.","jiṃ chanta gvāhāli yāye .","जिं छन्त ग्वाहालि याये ।"
"138","The door was left open.","lukhā cālā tala .","लुखा चाला तल ।"
"139","Someone knocked on the door.","sunānaṃ khāpā tvāthala .","सुनानं खापा त्वाथल ।"
"140","I like rice.","jita: jā ya: .","जित: जा य: ।"
"141","She likes spicy food.","vayāta masalā dugu nasā yaḥ .","वयात मसला दुगु नसा यः ।"
"142","He eats bread every day.","vaṃ nhiṃnhiṃ mari nai .","वं न्हिंन्हिं मरि नइ ।"
"143","We drink tea in the afternoon.","jimisaṃ nhinay cyā tvanā .","जिमिसं न्हिनय् च्या त्वना ।"
"144","They prefer coffee to tea.","imita cyā svayāṃ kaphi yaḥ .","इमित च्या स्वयां कफि यः ।"
"145","The soup is hot.","sūpa tāṃnvaḥ .","सूप तांन्वः ।"
"146","The milk is cold.","duru khvāuṃse cvaṃ .","दुरु ख्वाउँसे च्वं ।"
"147","The bread is fresh.","mari tājā .","मरि ताजा ।"
"148","The fruit is sweet.","sisābusā cāku .","सिसाबुसा चाकु ।"
"149","This food tastes delicious.","thva nasā sā: .","थ्व नसा सा: ।"
"150","I cook dinner every evening.","jiṃ nhyābaleṃ saniilay beli dayeke .","जिं न्ह्याबलें सनिइलय् बेलि दयेके ।"
"151","She is cooking lunch.","vaṃ lanca dayekā cvana .","वं लन्च दयेका च्वन ।"
"152","He boils water for tea.","vaṃ cyāyā niṃtiṃ laḥ kvakāi .","वं च्याया निंतिं लः क्वकाइ ।"
"153","We bake bread on Sundays.","jimisaṃ āitabārakhunhu mari seke yānā .","जिमिसं आइतबारखुन्हु मरि सेके याना ।"
"154","They fry the fish in oil.","imisaṃ nyāyāta cikaṃ pui .","इमिसं न्यायात चिकं पुइ ।"
"155","I cut the vegetables.","jiṃ tarakārī tvāthalā .","जिं तरकारी त्वाथला ।"
"156","She stirs the sauce.","vaṃ sasa saṃkala .","वं सस संकल ।"
"157","He adds salt to the soup.","vaṃ sūpay ci tala .","वं सूपय् चि तल ।"
"158","We mix the ingredients together.","jimisaṃ halaṃjvalaṃ nāpaṃ lvākachyānā .","जिमिसं हलंज्वलं नापं ल्वाकछ्याना ।"
"159","The chef prepares the meal.","śephaṃ nasā dayekī .","शेफं नसा दयेकी ।"
"160","I do not eat meat.","jiṃ lā manayā .","जिं ला मनया ।"
"161","She does not like onions.","vayāta pyāja mayaḥ .","वयात प्याज मयः ।"
"162","He never drinks alcohol.","vaṃ gubaleṃ aylāḥ tvanī makhu .","वं गुबलें अय्लाः त्वनी मखु ।"
"163","We do not add sugar.","jimisaṃ cinī matanā .","जिमिसं चिनी मतना ।"
"164","The water is not boiling yet.","laḥ āḥtakaṃ kvāḥlaḥ mavaḥni .","लः आःतकं क्वाःलः मवःनि ।"
"165","Do you like fish?","chanta nyā yaḥ lā ?","छन्त न्या यः ला ?"
"166","Does she eat vegetables?","vaṃ tarakārī nai lā ?","वं तरकारी नइ ला ?"
"167","What do you want to eat?","chanta chu naye māsti vaḥ ?","छन्त छु नये मास्ति वः ?"
"168","How much salt should I add?","jiṃ guli ci tayemāḥ ?","जिं गुलि चि तयेमाः ?"
"169","Is the food ready?","nasā tayāra jula lā ?","नसा तयार जुल ला ?"
"170","What is this dish called?","thva parikārayāta chu dhāi ?","थ्व परिकारयात छु धाइ ?"
"171","Would you like some more?","chanta megu chuṃ yaḥ lā ?","छन्त मेगु छुं यः ला ?"
"172","Can I have a glass of water?","jitaḥ chagū gilāsa laḥ tvane phai lā ?","जितः छगू गिलास लः त्वने फै ला ?"
"173","I cooked pasta last night.","jiṃ mhigaḥ cānhay pāstā dayekā .","जिं म्हिगः चान्हय् पास्ता दयेका ।"
"174","She made a cake for the party.","vaṃ bhvajayā niṃtiṃ keka dayekala .","वं भ्वजया निंतिं केक दयेकल ।"
"175","He ate too much.","vaṃ yakva nala .","वं यक्व नल ।"
"176","We had dinner at a restaurant.","jimisaṃ reṣṭurāṃy beli yānā .","जिमिसं रेष्टुराँय् बेलि याना ।"
"177","They brought wine to the dinner.","imisaṃ belii ay lāḥ hala .","इमिसं बेलिइ अय् लाः हल ।"
"178","I did not finish my meal.","jiṃ jigu nasā sidhayeke maphuta .","जिं जिगु नसा सिधयेके मफुत ।"
"179","The soup was too salty.","sūpa tasakaṃ ciluse cvaṃ .","सूप तसकं चिलुसे च्वं ।"
"180","Did you enjoy the food?","chanta nasā nhyaipula lā ?","छन्त नसा न्ह्यइपुल ला ?"
"181","What did she cook?","vaṃ chu dayekala ?","वं छु दयेकल ?"
"182","I will cook dinner tonight.","jiṃ thauṃ bahanī beli dayeke .","जिं थौं बहनी बेलि दयेके ।"
"183","She will bake a cake tomorrow.","vaṃ kanhay keka dayekī .","वं कन्हय् केक दयेकी ।"
"184","We will eat at a restaurant.","jimisaṃ reṣṭurāṃy nayegu jui .","जिमिसं रेष्टुराँय् नयेगु जुइ ।"
"185","Will you try this dish?","chiṃ thugu parikāra nayāsvayādī lā ?","छिं थुगु परिकार नयास्वयादी ला ?"
"186","I am going to cook fish.","jiṃ nyā jvare yāyetyanāgu du .","जिं न्या ज्वरे यायेत्यनागु दु ।"
"187","She has never tasted this fruit.","vaṃ thugu sisābusā gubaleṃ savāḥ makāni .","वं थुगु सिसाबुसा गुबलें सवाः मकानि ।"
"188","We have been cooking for two hours.","jimisaṃ nasā jvare yānāgu nighau dayedhuṃkala .","जिमिसं नसा ज्वरे यानागु निघौ दयेधुंकल ।"
"189","The food has gotten cold.","nasā khvāuṃse cvana .","नसा ख्वाउँसे च्वन ।"
"190","He had already eaten before we arrived.","jipiṃ thyane nhyaḥ he vaṃ nayedhuṃkala .","जिपिं थ्यने न्ह्यः हे वं नयेधुंकल ।"
"191","The bread is baked in a wood oven.","mari siṃyā obhanay seke yāi .","मरि सिँया ओभनय् सेके याइ ।"
"192","The fish was cooked by my grandmother.","nyā jimi ajiṃ dayekūgu khaḥ .","न्या जिमि अजिं दयेकूगु खः ।"
"193","The vegetables are washed before cooking.","tarakārī buke nhyaḥ silegu yāi .","तरकारी बुके न्ह्यः सिलेगु याइ ।"
"194","Rice is eaten in many countries.","yakva deśay jā nayegu yā.","यक्व देशय् जा नयेगु या।"
"195","The cake will be finished soon.","keka yākanaṃ sidhai .","केक याकनं सिधइ ।"
"196","Stir the sauce slowly.","sasa buluhuṃ saṃki .","सस बुलुहुं संकि ।"
"197","Do not burn the food!","nasā cyāke mate !","नसा च्याके मते !"
"198","Add a little salt.","bhacā ci ti .","भचा चि ति ।"
"199","Taste this and tell me what you think.","thva savāḥ kayādisaṃ ale chaṃ chu bicāḥ yānā dhakāḥ jitaḥ dhā .","थ्व सवाः कयादिसँ अले छं छु बिचाः याना धकाः जितः धा ।"
"200","Let us order dessert.","jhīsaṃ ḍejarṭa arḍara yāyenu .","झीसं डेजर्ट अर्डर यायेनु ।"
"201","If you add too much salt, the food will be ruined.","yakva ci tayābila dhāḥsā nasā syanī .","यक्व चि तयाबिल धाःसा नसा स्यनी ।"
"202","If I had more time, I would bake a cake.","jike yakva ī dugu khaḥsā, jiṃ keka seke yāyegu jui .","जिके यक्व ई दुगु खःसा, जिं केक सेके यायेगु जुइ ।"
"203","If she had bought the ingredients, we would have cooked.","vaṃ halaṃjvalaṃ nyāḥgu jūsā, jimisaṃ nasā jvare yāyegu jui .","वं हलंज्वलं न्याःगु जूसा, जिमिसं नसा ज्वरे यायेगु जुइ ।"
"204","You should eat more vegetables.","tarakārī apvaḥ nayemāḥ .","तरकारी अप्वः नयेमाः ।"
"205","We must wash the fruit before eating.","jhīsaṃ naye nhyaḥ sisābusā sile he māḥ .","झीसं नये न्ह्यः सिसाबुसा सिले हे माः ।"
"206","I can cook Italian food.","jiṃ iṭāliyana nasā dayeke phu .","जिं इटालियन नसा दयेके फु ।"
"207","She might bring dessert.","vaṃ ḍejarṭa hayephu .","वं डेजर्ट हयेफु ।"
"208","You need to chop the onions.","chaṃ pyāja kucā yāyemāḥ .","छं प्याज कुचा यायेमाः ।"
"209","This restaurant is better than that one.","thva reṣṭurāṃ va svayāṃ bāṃlāḥ .","थ्व रेष्टुराँ व स्वयां बांलाः ।"
"210","Her cooking is the best in the family.","cheṃjalay vayāgu nasā jvare yāyegu dakalay bāṃlāḥ .","छेँजलय् वयागु नसा ज्वरे यायेगु दकलय् बांलाः ।"
"211","This soup is spicier than the last one.","thva sūpa nhāpāyāgu svayāṃ masalā du .","थ्व सूप न्हापायागु स्वयां मसला दु ।"
"212","The freshest fish is at the morning market.","dakalay tājāgu nyā suthasiyā bajāray du .","दकलय् ताजागु न्या सुथसिया बजारय् दु ।"
"213","I want some water.","jita: la: mā: .","जित: ल: मा: ।"
"214","There is no sugar left.","ana cini lyaṃ madu .","अन चिनि ल्यं मदु ।"
"215","We need a few more eggs.","jhīta bhacā apvaḥ kheṃy māla .","झीत भचा अप्वः खेँय् माल ।"
"216","He ate all of the rice.","vaṃ dakvaṃ jā nala .","वं दक्वं जा नल ।"
"217","Is there any milk?","ana duru du lā ?","अन दुरु दु ला ?"
"218","The dish that she cooked was excellent.","vaṃ dayekūgu parikāra tasakaṃ bāṃlāḥ .","वं दयेकूगु परिकार तसकं बांलाः ।"
"219","The restaurant where we ate last night is famous.","jimisaṃ mhigaḥ cānhay nayegu reṣṭurenṭa nāṃjāḥ .","जिमिसं म्हिगः चान्हय् नयेगु रेष्टुरेन्ट नांजाः ।"
"220","The chef who trained in Paris cooks the best food.","perisay tālima kāḥmha śephaṃ dakalay bāṃlāḥgu nasā dayekī .","पेरिसय् तालिम काःम्ह शेफं दकलय् बांलाःगु नसा दयेकी ।"
"221","I eat rice and fish.","jiṃ jā va nyā nayā .","जिं जा व न्या नया ।"
"222","She likes coffee but not tea.","vayāta kaphī yaḥ tara cyā mayaḥ .","वयात कफी यः तर च्या मयः ।"
"223","He eats a lot because he is always hungry.","vaṃ yakva nai chāy dhāḥsā vayāta nhyābaleṃ naye pityāḥ .","वं यक्व नइ छाय् धाःसा वयात न्ह्याबलें नये पित्याः ।"
"224","I will cook dinner or we can order food.","jiṃ beli dayeke makhusā jhīsaṃ nasā arḍara yāyephu .","जिं बेलि दयेके मखुसा झीसं नसा अर्डर यायेफु ।"
"225","Although the food was expensive, it was delicious.","nasā thike jūsāṃ sāḥ .","नसा थिके जूसां साः ।"
"226","This is my plate.","thva jigu pleṭa khaḥ .","थ्व जिगु प्लेट खः ।"
"227","That glass is yours.","va gilāsa chaṃgu khaḥ .","व गिलास छंगु खः ।"
"228","Her recipe is very popular.","vayāgu resipi tasakaṃ nāṃjāḥ .","वयागु रेसिपि तसकं नांजाः ।"
"229","Our kitchen is small but clean.","jimigu bhutūkvathā cidhaṃ tara saphā .","जिमिगु भुतूक्वथा चिधं तर सफा ।"
"230","He said that the food was delicious.","vaṃ dhāla ki nasā sāḥ .","वं धाल कि नसा साः ।"
"231","She asked me if I wanted more rice.","vaṃ jikey nyana, jitaḥ apvaḥ jā māḥ lā dhakāḥ .","वं जिकेय् न्यन, जितः अप्वः जा माः ला धकाः ।"
"232","She cooks quickly.","vaṃ yākanaṃ nasā jvare yāi .","वं याकनं नसा ज्वरे याइ ।"
"233","I eat slowly.","jiṃ buluhuṃ naye .","जिं बुलुहुं नये ।"
"234","The food is almost ready.","nasā tayāra he juidhuṃkala .","नसा तयार हे जुइधुंकल ।"
"235","I usually eat at home.","jiṃ apvaḥ dhayā theṃ cheṃy he nayegu yānā .","जिं अप्वः धया थें छेँय् हे नयेगु याना ।"
"236","The vegetables need to be washed.","tarakārī silemāḥ .","तरकारी सिलेमाः ।"
"237","I have never eaten sushi.","jiṃ gubaleṃ suśi manayāni .","जिं गुबलें सुशि मनयानि ।"
"238","Shall we have some tea?","jhīsaṃ bhacā cyā tvanegu lā ?","झीसं भचा च्या त्वनेगु ला ?"
"239","Give me a piece of bread.","jitaḥ mari chakū byu .","जितः मरि छकू ब्यु ।"
"240","The dinner was cooked by my father.","beli jimi abuṃ dayekūgu khaḥ .","बेलि जिमि अबुं दयेकूगु खः ।"
"241","Who cooked this meal?","thva nasā sunāṃ dayekala ?","थ्व नसा सुनां दयेकल ?"
"242","Neither the soup nor the rice was good.","na sūpa bāṃlāḥ na jā .","न सूप बांलाः न जा ।"
"243","Both the meat and the fish are fresh.","lā va nyā nittāṃ tājā .","ला व न्या नित्तां ताजा ।"
"244","Not only did she cook, but she also cleaned.","vaṃ nasā jaka makhu, sucukucu naṃ yāta .","वं नसा जक मखु, सुचुकुचु नं यात ।"
"245","I had the chicken while he had the fish.","jikey khāyā lā dugu khaḥsā vayāke nyā dugu khaḥ .","जिकेय् खाया ला दुगु खःसा वयाके न्या दुगु खः ।"
"246","I wonder whether the cake is ready.","keka tayāra jula lā dhakāḥ ji ajū cāla .","केक तयार जुल ला धकाः जि अजू चाल ।"
"247","I travel by plane.","ji phay khataṃ yātrā yāye .","जि फय् खतं यात्रा याये ।"
"248","She takes the train to the capital.","vaṃ rājadhānī taka ṭrena kāla .","वं राजधानी तक ट्रेन काल ।"
"249","He drives a car.","vaṃ kāra cale yāi .","वं कार चले याइ ।"
"250","We fly to Paris every summer.","jipiṃ nhyābaleṃ tāllāṃ perisa bvayegu yānā .","जिपिं न्ह्याबलें ताल्लां पेरिस ब्वयेगु याना ।"
"251","They ride the bus to school.","ipiṃ bvanekuthii basa gayāḥ vana .","इपिं ब्वनेकुथिइ बस गयाः वन ।"
"252","The plane leaves at ten.","havāijahāja jhitāḥ ilay pihāṃ vanī .","हवाइजहाज झिताः इलय् पिहां वनी ।"
"253","The train arrives in twenty minutes.","ṭrena nīgū mineṭay thyaṃkaḥ vai .","ट्रेन नीगू मिनेटय् थ्यंकः वइ ।"
"254","The bus is always late.","basa nhyābaleṃ libāi .","बस न्ह्याबलें लिबाइ ।"
"255","I need a ticket.","jitaḥ ṭikaṭa māla .","जितः टिकट माल ।"
"256","She bought a one-way ticket.","vaṃ vana-ve ṭikaṭa nyāta .","वं वन-वे टिकट न्यात ।"
"257","We arrived at the airport early.","jipiṃ nhāpā he eyaraporṭay thyana .","जिपिं न्हापा हे एयरपोर्टय् थ्यन ।"
"258","They missed the flight.","imisaṃ phlāiṭa tvaḥphikala .","इमिसं फ्लाइट त्वःफिकल ।"
"259","He lost his passport.","vayā pāsaporṭa taṃkala .","वया पासपोर्ट तंकल ।"
"260","I packed my suitcase last night.","jiṃ mhigaḥ cānhay jigu suṭakesa pvaḥcinā .","जिं म्हिगः चान्हय् जिगु सुटकेस प्वःचिना ।"
"261","She checked into the hotel.","vaṃ hoṭalay ceka ina yāta .","वं होटलय् चेक इन यात ।"
"262","We stayed at a small hotel.","jipiṃ chagū cikicādhaṃgu hoṭalay cvanā .","जिपिं छगू चिकिचाधंगु होटलय् च्वना ।"
"263","The room was very comfortable.","kvathā tasakaṃ jyāchiṃ .","क्वथा तसकं ज्याछिं ।"
"264","I did not like the hotel.","jita: hoṭala maya: .","जित: होटल मय: ।"
"265","Did you enjoy your trip?","chigu yātrā nhyaipula lā ?","छिगु यात्रा न्ह्यइपुल ला ?"
"266","Where did you go on vacation?","cha chuṭṭīi gana vanāgu khaḥ ?","छ छुट्टीइ गन वनागु खः ?"
"267","I will travel to Japan next year.","ji megu daṃy jāpāna cāḥhile .","जि मेगु दँय् जापान चाःहिले ।"
"268","She is going to visit her family.","va vayā cheṃjaḥyāta nāpalāyeta vanetyaṃgu du .","व वया छेँजःयात नापलायेत वनेत्यंगु दु ।"
"269","We will take the train.","jipiṃ relaṃ cvane .","जिपिं रेलं च्वने ।"
"270","He will not fly because he is afraid.","va gyāḥguliṃ bvai makhu .","व ग्याःगुलिं ब्वइ मखु ।"
"271","Will they come with us?","ipiṃ jhī nāpaṃ vai lā ?","इपिं झी नापं वइ ला ?"
"272","When will the plane arrive?","havāijahāja gubalay thyanī ?","हवाइजहाज गुबलय् थ्यनी ?"
"273","I am waiting for the bus.","ji basa piyācvanā .","जि बस पियाच्वना ।"
"274","She is packing her bags.","vaṃ vayāgu mhicā pvaḥcinā cvana .","वं वयागु म्हिचा प्वःचिना च्वन ।"
"275","They are boarding the plane.","ipiṃ vimānay cvanācvaṃgu du .","इपिं विमानय् च्वनाच्वंगु दु ।"
"276","We were driving when it started to rain.","vā vayegu suru jūbalay jipiṃ ḍrāibha yānācvanāgu khaḥ .","वा वयेगु सुरु जूबलय् जिपिं ड्राइभ यानाच्वनागु खः ।"
"277","I have been to Italy three times.","ji svakaḥ iṭalī vanedhuna .","जि स्वकः इटली वनेधुन ।"
"278","She has never flown before.","vaṃ nhāpā gubaleṃ bvaye maphuni .","वं न्हापा गुबलें ब्वये मफुनि ।"
"279","We have already booked the hotel.","jimisaṃ hoṭala buka yāye dhuna .","जिमिसं होटल बुक याये धुन ।"
"280","They had already left when we arrived.","jipiṃ thyaṃbalay ipiṃ vanedhuṃkūgu khaḥ .","जिपिं थ्यंबलय् इपिं वनेधुंकूगु खः ।"
"281","He has been traveling for three months.","vaykaḥ yātrā yānādīgu svalā dayedhuṃkala .","वय्कः यात्रा यानादीगु स्वला दयेधुंकल ।"
"282","The ticket was bought online.","ṭikaṭa analāinapākheṃ nyāḥgu khaḥ .","टिकट अनलाइनपाखें न्याःगु खः ।"
"283","The flight was canceled.","uḍāna radda jūgu khaḥ .","उडान रद्द जूगु खः ।"
"284","Passengers are asked to show their passports.","yātrutayta pāsaporṭa kyaneta dhāḥgu du .","यात्रुतय्त पासपोर्ट क्यनेत धाःगु दु ।"
"285","The hotel room has been reserved.","hoṭalayā kvathā surakṣita yānātaḥgu du .","होटलया क्वथा सुरक्षित यानातःगु दु ।"
"286","Fasten your seatbelt.","chaṃgu siṭabelṭa cinābyu .","छंगु सिटबेल्ट चिनाब्यु ।"
"287","Do not leave your luggage unattended.","chaṃgu sāmāna sunāṃ naṃ masvase tvaḥte mate .","छंगु सामान सुनां नं मस्वसे त्वःते मते ।"
"288","Please show me your ticket.","jitaḥ chigu ṭikaṭa kyanādisaṃ .","जितः छिगु टिकट क्यनादिसँ ।"
"289","If we leave now, we will catch the train.","āḥ vana dhāḥsā rela kāye .","आः वन धाःसा रेल काये ।"
"290","If I had a car, I would drive there.","jike kāra dugu khaḥsā, jiṃ ana cale yāyegu jui .","जिके कार दुगु खःसा, जिं अन चले यायेगु जुइ ।"
"291","If they had arrived earlier, they would not have missed the flight.","ipiṃ nhāpā he thyaṃgu jūsā umisaṃ phlāiṭa tvaḥphikī makhu .","इपिं न्हापा हे थ्यंगु जूसा उमिसं फ्लाइट त्वःफिकी मखु ।"
"292","You must arrive two hours before the flight.","cha havāijahājayā nighau nhyaḥ he thyaṃke he māḥ .","छ हवाइजहाजया निघौ न्ह्यः हे थ्यंके हे माः ।"
"293","I should book the hotel in advance.","jiṃ hoṭala nhyaḥ he buka yāyemāḥ .","जिं होटल न्ह्यः हे बुक यायेमाः ।"
"294","We can take a taxi to the center.","jhīsaṃ senṭara taka ṭyāksi kāyephu .","झीसं सेन्टर तक ट्याक्सि कायेफु ।"
"295","She could not find her gate.","vaṃ vayāgu geṭa luike maphuta .","वं वयागु गेट लुइके मफुत ।"
"296","The train is faster than the bus.","basa svayāṃ rela yākanaṃ vanī .","बस स्वयां रेल याकनं वनी ।"
"297","Flying is the quickest way to travel.","bvayegu dhayāgu dakalay yākanaṃ yātrā yāyegu laṃpu khaḥ .","ब्वयेगु धयागु दकलय् याकनं यात्रा यायेगु लँपु खः ।"
"298","This hotel is more expensive than that one.","thva hoṭala va svayāṃ thike .","थ्व होटल व स्वयां थिके ।"
"299","It was the longest flight I have ever taken.","jiṃ āḥtaka kayāgu madhyay thva dakalay tāḥhākaḥgu uḍāna khaḥ .","जिं आःतक कयागु मध्यय् थ्व दकलय् ताःहाकःगु उडान खः ।"
"300","The airport is far from the city.","thva vimānasthala śahara svayā tāpā.","थ्व विमानस्थल शहर स्वया तापा।"
"301","The hotel is near the beach.","hoṭala samundra sithay lāḥ .","होटल समुन्द्र सिथय् लाः ।"
"302","Go straight and turn left.","tapyaṃka huṃ ale khavay huṃ .","तप्यंक हुँ अले खवय् हुँ ।"
"303","The station is across the street.","sṭeśana laṃ pārii du .","स्टेशन लँ पारिइ दु ।"
"304","The museum is between the park and the river.","saṃgrahālaya pārka va khusiyā dathui lāḥ .","संग्रहालय पार्क व खुसिया दथुइ लाः ।"
"305","She said that the flight was delayed.","vaṃ dhāla ki uḍāna libāta .","वं धाल कि उडान लिबात ।"
"306","He told me that the hotel was fully booked.","vaṃ jitaḥ dhāla ki hoṭala pūvaṃka buka juidhuṃkala .","वं जितः धाल कि होटल पूवंक बुक जुइधुंकल ।"
"307","They asked us where we were going.","jipiṃ gana vanetyanā dhakāḥ nyana .","जिपिं गन वनेत्यना धकाः न्यन ।"
"308","All passengers must board now.","sakaleṃ yātruta āḥ he cvane he māḥ .","सकलें यात्रुत आः हे च्वने हे माः ।"
"309","Some flights were canceled.","chuṃ chuṃ uḍāna radda jūgu khaḥ .","छुं छुं उडान रद्द जूगु खः ।"
"310","No one was at the station.","sṭeśanay suṃ naṃ madu .","स्टेशनय् सुं नं मदु ।"
"311","I have not seen any taxis.","jiṃ chuṃ naṃ ṭyāksī makhanāni .","जिं छुं नं ट्याक्सी मखनानि ।"
"312","The city where we stayed was beautiful.","jipiṃ cvanāgu śahara bāṃlāḥ .","जिपिं च्वनागु शहर बांलाः ।"
"313","The woman who sat next to me on the plane was very kind.","havāijahājay jigu likka cvanācvaṃmha misā tasakaṃ dayālu .","हवाइजहाजय् जिगु लिक्क च्वनाच्वंम्ह मिसा तसकं दयालु ।"
"314","We can take the bus or walk.","jhīsaṃ basaṃ bāy nyāsi vanephu .","झीसं बसं बाय् न्यासि वनेफु ।"
"315","I want to travel, but I do not have enough money.","jitaḥ cāḥhile māsti vaḥ, tara jikey gākka dhyabā madu .","जितः चाःहिले मास्ति वः, तर जिकेय् गाक्क ध्यबा मदु ।"
"316","She travels a lot because she loves exploring new places.","vaṃ yakva cāḥhilī chāy dhāḥsā vayāta nhūgu thāy mālegu yaḥ .","वं यक्व चाःहिली छाय् धाःसा वयात न्हूगु थाय् मालेगु यः ।"
"317","Although the journey was long, we enjoyed it.","yātrā tāḥhākaḥ jūsāṃ jimisaṃ nhyaipukā .","यात्रा ताःहाकः जूसां जिमिसं न्ह्यइपुका ।"
"318","How long does the flight take?","uḍāna guli ī kāi ?","उडान गुलि ई काइ ?"
"319","I would like to rent a car.","jitaḥ kāra bālaṃ kāye māsti vaḥ .","जितः कार बालं काये मास्ति वः ।"
"320","The taxi driver took us to the wrong address.","ṭyāksī cālakaṃ jimita makhugu thāsay yaṃkala .","ट्याक्सी चालकं जिमित मखुगु थासय् यंकल ।"
"321","We are lost.","jipiṃ tana .","जिपिं तन ।"
"322","Can you tell me how to get to the train station?","rela sṭeśanay gathe yānāḥ thyaṃkegu dhakāḥ chiṃ jitaḥ dhayādī phai lā ?","रेल स्टेशनय् गथे यानाः थ्यंकेगु धकाः छिं जितः धयादी फै ला ?"
"323","I have never been here before.","ji thana nhāpā gubaleṃ mavanāni .","जि थन न्हापा गुबलें मवनानि ।"
"324","Excuse me, where is the nearest bus stop?","kṣamā yānādisaṃ, dakalay sattigu basa sṭapa gana du ?","क्षमा यानादिसँ, दकलय् सत्तिगु बस स्टप गन दु ?"
"325","My mother is a doctor.","jimi māṃ ḍākṭara khaḥ .","जिमि मां डाक्टर खः ।"
"326","His father works in a factory.","vayā abu kārakhānāy jyā yāimha khaḥ .","वया अबु कारखानाय् ज्या याइम्ह खः ।"
"327","She has two brothers and one sister.","vayā nimha kijā va chamha keheṃ du .","वया निम्ह किजा व छम्ह केहें दु ।"
"328","We live with our grandparents.","jipiṃ jimi ajāajipiṃ nāpa cvanā .","जिपिं जिमि अजाअजिपिं नाप च्वना ।"
"329","Their family is very large.","imigu cheṃjaḥ tasakaṃ taḥdhaṃ .","इमिगु छेँजः तसकं तःधं ।"
"330","The baby is sleeping.","macā dyanācvana .","मचा द्यनाच्वन ।"
"331","My oldest brother is married.","jimi taḥdhikaḥmha kijā ihipāḥ juidhuṃkala .","जिमि तःधिकःम्ह किजा इहिपाः जुइधुंकल ।"
"332","Her younger sister studies at the university.","vayā cīdhimha keheṃ viśvavidyālayay bvanācvana .","वया चीधिम्ह केहें विश्वविद्यालयय् ब्वनाच्वन ।"
"333","I love my parents.","jiṃ jimi māṃabuyāta matinā yānā .","जिं जिमि मांअबुयात मतिना याना ।"
"334","He takes care of his grandmother.","vaṃ thaḥ ajiyāta bicāḥ yāi .","वं थः अजियात बिचाः याइ ।"
"335","Our house has three bedrooms.","jimigu cheṃy svakū dyanekvathā du .","जिमिगु छेँय् स्वकू द्यनेक्वथा दु ।"
"336","The living room is next to the kitchen.","baiṭhaka kvathā bhutūkvathāyā likkasaṃ du .","बैठक क्वथा भुतूक्वथाया लिक्कसं दु ।"
"337","There is a garden behind the house.","cheṃyā liune kyaba du .","छेँया लिउने क्यब दु ।"
"338","The bathroom is upstairs.","bātharuma cvay du .","बाथरुम च्वय् दु ।"
"339","We painted the walls last month.","jimisaṃ vaṃgu lāy aṃgalay uṃ pānā .","जिमिसं वंगु लाय् अंगलय् उं पाना ।"
"340","She decorated her room.","vaṃ vayāgu kvathā samāḥ yāta .","वं वयागु क्वथा समाः यात ।"
"341","The roof needs to be repaired.","pali bhiṃke māḥgu du .","पलि भिंके माःगु दु ।"
"342","I want to buy a new sofa.","jitaḥ nhūgu sophā nyāye māsti vaḥ .","जितः न्हूगु सोफा न्याये मास्ति वः ।"
"343","He built a bookshelf himself.","vaṃ thamhaṃ he saphūyā takhtā dayekala .","वं थम्हं हे सफूया तख्ता दयेकल ।"
"344","The house was built fifty years ago.","thva cheṃ nyaydaṃ nhyaḥ dayekūgu khaḥ .","थ्व छेँ न्यय्दँ न्ह्यः दयेकूगु खः ।"
"345","I do not have any siblings.","jimi suṃ naṃ dājukijā taḥkeheṃ madu .","जिमि सुं नं दाजुकिजा तःकेहें मदु ।"
"346","She does not live with her parents.","va vayā māṃabulise cvanī makhu .","व वया मांअबुलिसे च्वनी मखु ।"
"347","He is not married.","vaṃ ihipā yāḥgu maduni .","वं इहिपा याःगु मदुनि ।"
"348","We have never moved to another city.","jipiṃ gubaleṃ megu śaharay vanāgu maduni .","जिपिं गुबलें मेगु शहरय् वनागु मदुनि ।"
"349","How many children do you have?","chimi gvaḥmha masta du ?","छिमि ग्वःम्ह मस्त दु ?"
"350","Where does your family live?","chimi cheṃjaḥ gana cvanī ?","छिमि छेँजः गन च्वनी ?"
"351","Do you have any pets?","chike lahinātapiṃ janāvarata du lā ?","छिके लहिनातपिं जनावरत दु ला ?"
"352","Is your father at home?","chimi abu cheṃy du lā ?","छिमि अबु छेँय् दु ला ?"
"353","Who is the oldest in your family?","chimi cheṃjalay dakalay taḥdhikaḥmha su khaḥ ?","छिमि छेँजलय् दकलय् तःधिकःम्ह सु खः ?"
"354","I grew up in a small town.","ji chagū cidhaṃgu śaharay bvalaṃmha khaḥ .","जि छगू चिधंगु शहरय् ब्वलंम्ह खः ।"
"355","My parents met at university.","jimi māṃabu viśvavidyālayay nāpa lāta .","जिमि मांअबु विश्वविद्यालयय् नाप लात ।"
"356","She was born in nineteen ninety.","va jhiṃnyādaṃy būgu khaḥ .","व झिंन्यादँय् बूगु खः ।"
"357","We moved to this city five years ago.","jipiṃ nyādaṃ nhyaḥ thugu śaharay cvaṃ vayāgu khaḥ .","जिपिं न्यादँ न्ह्यः थुगु शहरय् च्वं वयागु खः ।"
"358","They got married last summer.","imisaṃ vaṃgu tāllāy ihipāḥ yāta .","इमिसं वंगु ताल्लाय् इहिपाः यात ।"
"359","My sister will have a baby next month.","jimi keheṃyā megu lāy macā dai .","जिमि केहेंया मेगु लाय् मचा दइ ।"
"360","We will visit our grandparents this weekend.","thugu vāḥyā lipatay jipiṃ jimi ajāajiyāta nāpalāyeta vane .","थुगु वाःया लिपतय् जिपिं जिमि अजाअजियात नापलायेत वने ।"
"361","He is going to move to a new apartment.","va nhūgu apārṭamenṭay vanetyaṃgu du .","व न्हूगु अपार्टमेन्टय् वनेत्यंगु दु ।"
"362","They will celebrate their anniversary.","imisaṃ thaḥgu vārṣikotsava nyāyekī .","इमिसं थःगु वार्षिकोत्सव न्यायेकी ।"
"363","If we had a bigger house, we would adopt a dog.","jimike taḥkūgu cheṃ dugu khaḥsā, jimisaṃ khicāyāta thaḥmhaṃ tayegu jui .","जिमिके तःकूगु छेँ दुगु खःसा, जिमिसं खिचायात थःम्हं तयेगु जुइ ।"
"364","If my grandparents were alive, they would be proud.","jimi ajāajipiṃ mvānācvaṃgu jūsā imisaṃ garva yāigu khaḥ .","जिमि अजाअजिपिं म्वानाच्वंगु जूसा इमिसं गर्व याइगु खः ।"
"365","Children must respect their parents.","mastay saṃ thaḥ māṃabuyāta sammāna yāyemāḥ .","मस्तय् सं थः मांअबुयात सम्मान यायेमाः ।"
"366","You should call your mother.","chaṃ chimi māṃyāta saḥtemāḥ .","छं छिमि मांयात सःतेमाः ।"
"367","I can hear the baby crying.","jiṃ macā khvayācvaṃgu nyanephu .","जिं मचा ख्वयाच्वंगु न्यनेफु ।"
"368","Close the door, please.","lukhā tinā disaṃ .","लुखा तिना दिसँ ।"
"369","Do not make so much noise.","uli saḥ pikāye mate .","उलि सः पिकाये मते ।"
"370","Turn off the lights.","mata syānābyu .","मत स्यानाब्यु ।"
"371","My house is bigger than yours.","jigu cheṃ chaṃgu svayāḥ ta:ku .","जिगु छेँ छंगु स्वयाः त:कु ।"
"372","She is taller than her brother.","va vayā kijā svayāṃ taḥdhikaḥ .","व वया किजा स्वयां तःधिकः ।"
"373","He is the youngest child in the family.","va cheṃjaḥyā dakalay cīdhimha macā khaḥ .","व छेँजःया दकलय् चीधिम्ह मचा खः ।"
"374","This is the most beautiful garden I have ever seen.","thva jiṃ āḥtaka khanāgu kyaba madhyay dakalay bāṃlāḥgu khaḥ .","थ्व जिं आःतक खनागु क्यब मध्यय् दकलय् बांलाःगु खः ।"
"375","The children are raised by their grandparents.","mastayta imi ajāajipinsaṃ bvalaṃkācvaṃgu du .","मस्तय्त इमि अजाअजिपिन्सं ब्वलंकाच्वंगु दु ।"
"376","The dinner table was set by my sister.","ḍinara ṭebula jimi keheṃṃ dayekā bila .","डिनर टेबुल जिमि केहेंं दयेका बिल ।"
"377","The woman who lives upstairs is my aunt.","cvay cvaṃmha misā jimi māju khaḥ .","च्वय् च्वंम्ह मिसा जिमि माजु खः ।"
"378","The house where I was born is very old.","ji būgu cheṃ tasakaṃ pulāṃ .","जि बूगु छेँ तसकं पुलां ।"
"379","The children dress themselves.","mastay saṃ thaḥthamhaṃ vasaḥ punī .","मस्तय् सं थःथम्हं वसः पुनी ।"
"380","My parents love each other.","jimi māṃabuṃ thaḥthavay matinā yāi .","जिमि मांअबुं थःथवय् मतिना याइ ।"
"381","I miss my family.","jiṃ jimi cheṃjaḥyāta lumaṃkā .","जिं जिमि छेँजःयात लुमंका ।"
"382","She looks like her mother.","va vayā māṃ theṃ cvaṃ .","व वया मां थें च्वं ।"
"383","He inherited the house from his uncle.","vaṃ vayā kakāpākheṃ cheṃ uttarādhikāray kāḥgu khaḥ .","वं वया ककापाखें छेँ उत्तराधिकारय् काःगु खः ।"
"384","We named the baby after my grandfather.","jimisaṃ macāyā nāṃ jimi ajāyā nāmaṃ tayā .","जिमिसं मचाया नां जिमि अजाया नामं तया ।"
"385","Everyone in our family speaks two languages.","jimi cheṃjalay sakasinaṃ nigū bhāy lhāi .","जिमि छेँजलय् सकसिनं निगू भाय् ल्हाइ ।"
"386","My home is where my family is.","jigu cheṃ dhayāgu jimi cheṃjaḥpiṃ duthāy khaḥ .","जिगु छेँ धयागु जिमि छेँजःपिं दुथाय् खः ।"
"387","As a child, I used to play in the garden.","macābalay ji kyabay mhitegu yānā .","मचाबलय् जि क्यबय् म्हितेगु याना ।"
"388","The neighbors are very kind to us.","jaḥlākhaḥlāpiṃ jimita tasakaṃ dayālu .","जःलाखःलापिं जिमित तसकं दयालु ।"
"389","I work in an office.","ji jyākuthii jyā yānā .","जि ज्याकुथिइ ज्या याना ।"
"390","She studies mathematics.","vaṃ gaṇita bvanī .","वं गणित ब्वनी ।"
"391","He is a teacher.","vaykaḥ guru khaḥ .","वय्कः गुरु खः ।"
"392","We have a meeting at three.","jimigu svatāḥ ilay muṃjyā du .","जिमिगु स्वताः इलय् मुंज्या दु ।"
"393","They study together every day.","ipiṃ nhiṃ nhiṃ nāpaṃ bvanī .","इपिं न्हिं न्हिं नापं ब्वनी ।"
"394","The students are in the classroom.","bvanāmipiṃ bvanekvathāy du .","ब्वनामिपिं ब्वनेक्वथाय् दु ।"
"395","My boss is very strict.","jimi hākima tasakaṃ kaḍā .","जिमि हाकिम तसकं कडा ।"
"396","She teaches English and French.","vaṃ aṃgreji va phrenca syanī .","वं अंग्रेजि व फ्रेन्च स्यनी ।"
"397","He writes reports every week.","vaṃ vāḥpatikaṃ riporṭa cvai .","वं वाःपतिकं रिपोर्ट च्वइ ।"
"398","I have an exam tomorrow.","jigu kanhay jāṃca du .","जिगु कन्हय् जाँच दु ।"
"399","I started a new job last month.","jiṃ vaṃgu lāy nhūgu jyā suru yānā .","जिं वंगु लाय् न्हूगु ज्या सुरु याना ।"
"400","She graduated from university in June.","vaṃ junay viśvavidyālaya pāsa yāta .","वं जुनय् विश्वविद्यालय पास यात ।"
"401","He was promoted to manager.","vayāta myānejaray baḍhuvā yāta .","वयात म्यानेजरय् बढुवा यात ।"
"402","We finished the project on time.","jimisaṃ ilay he projekṭa sidhayekā .","जिमिसं इलय् हे प्रोजेक्ट सिधयेका ।"
"403","They did not pass the test.","ipiṃ jāṃcay pāsa majū .","इपिं जाँचय् पास मजू ।"
"404","I will apply for the position.","jiṃ padayā niṃtiṃ āvedana bī .","जिं पदया निंतिं आवेदन बी ।"
"405","She is going to start university.","vaṃ viśvavidyālaya suru yāyetyaṃgu du .","वं विश्वविद्यालय सुरु यायेत्यंगु दु ।"
"406","He will give a presentation next Monday.","vaykalaṃ vaigu somabāra prastutikaraṇa biyādī .","वय्कलं वइगु सोमबार प्रस्तुतिकरण बियादी ।"
"407","We will hire more employees.","jimisaṃ yakva jyākaḥmita kāye .","जिमिसं यक्व ज्याकःमित काये ।"
"408","Will you take the job?","chaṃ jyā kāye lā ?","छं ज्या काये ला ?"
"409","I am studying for an exam.","ji jāṃcayā niṃtiṃ bvanācvanā .","जि जाँचया निंतिं ब्वनाच्वना ।"
"410","She is working on a new project.","vaṃ nhūgu projekṭay jyā yānācvana .","वं न्हूगु प्रोजेक्टय् ज्या यानाच्वन ।"
"411","They are having a meeting right now.","āḥ thattheṃ imigu muṃjyā cvanācvaṃgu du .","आः थत्थें इमिगु मुँज्या च्वनाच्वंगु दु ।"
"412","I have worked here for ten years.","jiṃ thana jhidaṃ taka jyā yānāgu du .","जिं थन झिदँ तक ज्या यानागु दु ।"
"413","She has already finished her homework.","vaṃ vayāgu homavarka sidhayeke dhuṃkala .","वं वयागु होमवर्क सिधयेके धुंकल ।"
"414","He has been studying all day.","vaṃ nhicchi he bvanācvaṃgu du .","वं न्हिच्छि हे ब्वनाच्वंगु दु ।"
"415","We had already started when the boss arrived.","hākima thyaṃbalay jimisaṃ jyā nhyāke dhuṃkūgu khaḥ .","हाकिम थ्यंबलय् जिमिसं ज्या न्ह्याके धुंकूगु खः ।"
"416","I do not understand this problem.","jiṃ thugu samasyā mathula .","जिं थुगु समस्या मथुल ।"
"417","She does not agree with the plan.","va yojanālise sahamata majū .","व योजनालिसे सहमत मजू ।"
"418","He cannot come to the meeting.","va baiṭhakay vaye phai makhu .","व बैठकय् वये फै मखु ।"
"419","What do you do for a living?","chaṃ chu jyā yāna?","छँ छु ज्या यान?"
"420","Where does she work?","vaṃ gana jyā yāi ?","वं गन ज्या याइ ?"
"421","Did you finish the report?","chaṃ riporṭa sidhayekā lā ?","छं रिपोर्ट सिधयेका ला ?"
"422","How was the exam?","jāṃca gathe jula ?","जाँच गथे जुल ?"
"423","Who is your teacher?","chimi guru su khaḥ ?","छिमि गुरु सु खः ?"
"424","The homework was assigned yesterday.","homavarka mhigaḥ bila .","होमवर्क म्हिगः बिल ।"
"425","The results will be announced next week.","licvaḥ megu vālay sārvajanika jui .","लिच्वः मेगु वालय् सार्वजनिक जुइ ।"
"426","English is taught in many schools.","yakva bvanekuthii aṃgrejī bvaṃkī .","यक्व ब्वनेकुथिइ अंग्रेजी ब्वंकी ।"
"427","The report must be submitted by Friday.","prativedana śukravāḥ dune bujhe yāyemāḥ .","प्रतिवेदन शुक्रवाः दुने बुझे यायेमाः ।"
"428","If I study hard, I will pass the exam.","jiṃ bāṃlāka bvanāgu khaḥsā ji jāṃcay pāsa jui .","जिं बांलाक ब्वनागु खःसा जि जाँचय् पास जुइ ।"
"429","If she had studied more, she would have passed.","vaṃ apvaḥ bvaṃgu jūsā, va pāsa juigu khaḥ .","वं अप्वः ब्वंगु जूसा, व पास जुइगु खः ।"
"430","You must finish your homework.","chaṃ chaṃgu homavarka sidhayeke he māḥ .","छं छंगु होमवर्क सिधयेके हे माः ।"
"431","I should study harder.","jiṃ ajha mehanata yānāḥ bvanemāḥ .","जिं अझ मेहनत यानाः ब्वनेमाः ।"
"432","She can speak three languages.","vaṃ svaṃgū bhāy lhāye phu .","वं स्वंगू भाय् ल्हाये फु ।"
"433","May I leave early today?","ji thauṃ nhāpāṃ he vane phai lā ?","जि थौं न्हापां हे वने फै ला ?"
"434","Open your books to page ten.","chaṃgu saphū pau jhigūi cāyeki .","छंगु सफू पौ झिगूइ चायेकि ।"
"435","Do not talk during the exam.","jāṃcayā ilay khaṃ lhāye mate .","जाँचया इलय् खँ ल्हाये मते ।"
"436","Study the first three chapters.","nhāpāṃgu svaṃgū adhyāya adhyayana yā .","न्हापांगु स्वंगू अध्याय अध्ययन या ।"
"437","This problem is harder than the last one.","thva samasyā lipāṃgu svayāṃ thāku .","थ्व समस्या लिपांगु स्वयां थाकु ।"
"438","She is the best student in the class.","va kakṣāy dakalay bāṃlāḥmha bvanāmi khaḥ .","व कक्षाय् दकलय् बांलाःम्ह ब्वनामि खः ।"
"439","Working from home is easier than going to the office.","jyākuthii vanegu svayāṃ cheṃy cvanāḥ jyā yāyegu aḥpu .","ज्याकुथिइ वनेगु स्वयां छेँय् च्वनाः ज्या यायेगु अःपु ।"
"440","The teacher who helped me is retiring.","jitaḥ gvāhāli yāḥmha guru avakāśa kāyetyaṃgu du .","जितः ग्वाहालि याःम्ह गुरु अवकाश कायेत्यंगु दु ।"
"441","The school where I studied has closed.","jiṃ bvanāgu bvanekuthi banda jula .","जिं ब्वनागु ब्वनेकुथि बन्द जुल ।"
"442","I work hard and I study at night.","jiṃ tasakaṃ jyā yānā ale jiṃ bahanī bvanā .","जिं तसकं ज्या याना अले जिं बहनी ब्वना ।"
"443","She is tired but she keeps studying.","va tyānula tara vaṃ bvanā he cvana .","व त्यानुल तर वं ब्वना हे च्वन ।"
"444","He works because he needs money.","vaṃ jyā yāi chāy dhāḥsā vayāta dhyabā māḥ .","वं ज्या याइ छाय् धाःसा वयात ध्यबा माः ।"
"445","My colleague is on vacation.","jimi pāsā bidāy du .","जिमि पासा बिदाय् दु ।"
"446","The deadline is next Friday.","myāda vaigu śukravāḥ khaḥ .","म्याद वइगु शुक्रवाः खः ।"
"447","I have too much work to do.","jike yāyemāḥgu jyā yakva du .","जिके यायेमाःगु ज्या यक्व दु ।"
"448","She earned a promotion.","vaṃ padonnati kame yāta .","वं पदोन्नति कमे यात ।"
"449","The office is closed on weekends.","vāḥyā lipatay jyākuthi banda jui .","वाःया लिपतय् ज्याकुथि बन्द जुइ ।"
"450","I applied for the scholarship.","jiṃ chātravr̥ttiyā niṃtiṃ āvedana biyā .","जिं छात्रवृत्तिया निंतिं आवेदन बिया ।"
"451","Education is important for everyone.","śikṣā sakasitaṃ mahatvapūrṇa jui .","शिक्षा सकसितं महत्वपूर्ण जुइ ।"
"452","He was fired from his job.","vayāta jāgiraṃ likāḥgu khaḥ .","वयात जागिरं लिकाःगु खः ।"
"453","I am looking for a new job.","jiṃ nhūgu lajagāḥ mālācvanā .","जिं न्हूगु लजगाः मालाच्वना ।"
"454","What time does the class start?","kakṣā guli ilay nhyāi ?","कक्षा गुलि इलय् न्ह्याइ ?"
"455","The sun is shining.","nibhāḥ tvayācvaṃgu du .","निभाः त्वयाच्वंगु दु ।"
"456","It is raining.","vā vayācvaṃgu du .","वा वयाच्वंगु दु ।"
"457","It is very cold today.","thauṃ tasakaṃ khvāuṃse cvaṃ .","थौं तसकं ख्वाउँसे च्वं ।"
"458","The sky is blue.","sargaḥ vaṃcuse cvaṃ .","सर्गः वँचुसे च्वं ।"
"459","The wind is strong.","phay tasakaṃ vayācvaṃgu du .","फय् तसकं वयाच्वंगु दु ।"
"460","There are many trees in the forest.","jaṃgalay yakva simā du .","जंगलय् यक्व सिमा दु ।"
"461","The river flows to the sea.","thva khusi samudray bāḥ vanī.","थ्व खुसि समुद्रय् बाः वनी।"
"462","The mountains are covered with snow.","guṃcvay cvāpuṃ bhunātaḥgu du .","गुँच्वय् च्वापुं भुनातःगु दु ।"
"463","The flowers bloom in spring.","svāṃ basanta r̥tui hvaḥ .","स्वां बसन्त ऋतुइ ह्वः ।"
"464","The leaves fall in autumn.","śarada r̥tui haḥ kutuṃ vai .","शरद ऋतुइ हः कुतुं वइ ।"
"465","It rained all day yesterday.","mhigaḥ nhicchi he vā vala .","म्हिगः न्हिच्छि हे वा वल ।"
"466","The storm destroyed several houses.","vāphasaṃ āpālaṃ cheṃta syaṃkūgu du .","वाफसं आपालं छेँत स्यंकूगु दु ।"
"467","The lake was frozen last winter.","vaṃgu cikulāṃ pukhū khvāuṃse cvana .","वंगु चिकुलां पुखू ख्वाउँसे च्वन ।"
"468","We saw a rainbow after the rain.","jimisaṃ vā vayedhuṃkāḥ lāy lāmā khanā .","जिमिसं वा वयेधुंकाः लाय् लामा खना ।"
"469","The temperature dropped below zero.","tāpakrama śūnya svayāṃ cvay kuhāṃ vana .","तापक्रम शून्य स्वयां च्वय् कुहां वन ।"
"470","It will snow tomorrow.","kanhay cvāpu gāi .","कन्हय् च्वापु गाइ ।"
"471","The weather will be warm next week.","megu vālay mausama lumui .","मेगु वालय् मौसम लुमुइ ।"
"472","Spring will come soon.","basanta r̥tu yākanaṃ vai .","बसन्त ऋतु याकनं वइ ।"
"473","Will it rain this afternoon?","thauṃ nhinay vā vai lā ?","थौं न्हिनय् वा वइ ला ?"
"474","It is not raining.","vā vayācvaṃgu madu .","वा वयाच्वंगु मदु ।"
"475","The water is not clean.","laḥ saphā majū .","लः सफा मजू ।"
"476","There are no clouds in the sky.","sargatay supāṃy madu .","सर्गतय् सुपाँय् मदु ।"
"477","What is the weather like today?","thauṃyā mausama gathe cvaṃ ?","थौंया मौसम गथे च्वं ?"
"478","Is it going to rain?","vā vai lā ?","वा वइ ला ?"
"479","How cold is it outside?","pine guli khvāuṃ ?","पिने गुलि ख्वाउँ ?"
"480","Where does this river lead?","thva khusi gana vanī ?","थ्व खुसि गन वनी ?"
"481","Have you ever seen a volcano?","chaṃ gubaleṃ jvālāmukhī khanāgu du lā ?","छं गुबलें ज्वालामुखी खनागु दु ला ?"
"482","You should bring an umbrella.","chaṃ kusā jvanāvayemāḥ .","छं कुसा ज्वनावयेमाः ।"
"483","It might rain later.","lipā vā vayephu .","लिपा वा वयेफु ।"
"484","We must protect the environment.","jhīsaṃ vātāvaraṇa saṃrakṣaṇa yāyemāḥ .","झीसं वातावरण संरक्षण यायेमाः ।"
"485","Animals cannot survive without water.","laḥ madayekaṃ janāvarata mvāye phai makhu .","लः मदयेकं जनावरत म्वाये फै मखु ।"
"486","The forest was destroyed by fire.","miṃ nayāḥ jaṃgala dhvasta jula .","मिं नयाः जंगल ध्वस्त जुल ।"
"487","The island is surrounded by water.","thva ṭāpu lakhaṃ ghereyānātaḥgu du.","थ्व टापु लखं घेरेयानातःगु दु।"
"488","Many animals are endangered.","yakva janāvarata lopa juityaṃgu du .","यक्व जनावरत लोप जुइत्यंगु दु ।"
"489","Summer is hotter than spring.","basanta r̥tu svayāṃ tāllā apvaḥ tāṃnvaḥ .","बसन्त ऋतु स्वयां ताल्ला अप्वः तांन्वः ।"
"490","This is the tallest tree in the park.","thva pārkayā dakalay tajāḥgu simā khaḥ .","थ्व पार्कया दकलय् तजाःगु सिमा खः ।"
"491","The ocean is deeper than any lake.","samudra guguṃ naṃ pukhū svayāṃ tajāḥ .","समुद्र गुगुं नं पुखू स्वयां तजाः ।"
"492","Mount Everest is the highest mountain in the world.","māunṭa ebhareṣṭa halimayā dakalay tajāḥgu cvāpu khaḥ .","माउन्ट एभरेष्ट हलिमया दकलय् तजाःगु च्वापु खः ।"
"493","If it rains, we will stay inside.","vā vaḥsā jipiṃ dune cvane .","वा वःसा जिपिं दुने च्वने ।"
"494","If the ice melts, the sea level will rise.","cvāpu nāla dhāḥsā samudrayā sataha thahāṃ vanī.","च्वापु नाल धाःसा समुद्रया सतह थहां वनी।"
"495","If we do not protect nature, many species will disappear.","jhīsaṃ prakr̥tiyā rakṣā mayāḥsā yakva prajāti lopa jui .","झीसं प्रकृतिया रक्षा मयाःसा यक्व प्रजाति लोप जुइ ।"
"496","The sun sets and the moon rises.","nibhāḥ bī ale timilā pihāṃ vai .","निभाः बी अले तिमिला पिहां वइ ।"
"497","It is cold but sunny.","khvāuṃse cvaṃ tara nibhāḥ tvaḥ .","ख्वाउँसे च्वं तर निभाः त्वः ।"
"498","I like summer because the days are long.","jita: tāllā ya: chāy dhā:sā nhi tāḥhākaḥ jui .","जित: ताल्ला य: छाय् धा:सा न्हि ताःहाकः जुइ ।"
"499","The mountain that we climbed was very high.","jipiṃ gayāgu guṃcva tasakaṃ tajāḥ .","जिपिं गयागु गुँच्व तसकं तजाः ।"
"500","The river where we swam was crystal clear.","jipiṃ lāla kāḥgu khusi yacuse cvaṃ .","जिपिं लाल काःगु खुसि यचुसे च्वं ।"
"501","The air smells fresh after the rain.","vā vayedhuṃkāḥ phay tājā bāsnā vai .","वा वयेधुंकाः फय् ताजा बास्ना वइ ।"
"502","Birds sing in the morning.","jhaṃgaḥtaseṃ suthay me hālī .","झंगःतसें सुथय् मे हाली ।"
"503","The garden is full of flowers.","kyaba svāṃmāṃ jāḥ .","क्यब स्वांमां जाः ।"
"504","A strong earthquake shook the city.","tacvaḥgu bhukhācaṃ śahara saṃkala .","तच्वःगु भुखाचं शहर संकल ।"
"505","The sunset was beautiful.","nibhāḥ bībalay bāṃlāḥ .","निभाः बीबलय् बांलाः ।"
"506","Snow covers the ground in winter.","cikulābalay cvāpuṃ baṃ tvapui .","चिकुलाबलय् च्वापुं बँ त्वपुइ ।"
"507","The weather has been very hot this week.","thugu vālay mausama tasakaṃ tāṃnvaḥgu du .","थुगु वालय् मौसम तसकं तांन्वःगु दु ।"
"508","It hardly ever snows here.","thana sāyada he gubaleṃ cvāpu gāi .","थन सायद हे गुबलें च्वापु गाइ ।"
"509","Lightning struck the old tree.","pulāṃgu simāy palpasā nyāta .","पुलांगु सिमाय् पल्पसा न्यात ।"
"510","The tide comes in twice a day.","nhiṃ nikvaḥ jvārabhāṭā vai .","न्हिं निक्वः ज्वारभाटा वइ ।"
"511","I have a headache.","jitaḥ chyaṃ syānācvana .","जितः छ्यं स्यानाच्वन ।"
"512","She is sick.","va mhaṃ maphu .","व म्हं मफु ।"
"513","He broke his arm.","vaṃ vayāgu lhāḥ tvāḥdala .","वं वयागु ल्हाः त्वाःदल ।"
"514","My stomach hurts.","jigu pvāḥ syāta .","जिगु प्वाः स्यात ।"
"515","The doctor examined the patient.","ḍākṭaraṃ lvagiyā jāṃca yāta .","डाक्टरं ल्वगिया जाँच यात ।"
"516","We need to go to the hospital.","jhīpiṃ aspatāla vanemāḥgu du .","झीपिं अस्पताल वनेमाःगु दु ।"
"517","They gave him medicine.","imisaṃ vayāta vāsaḥ bila .","इमिसं वयात वासः बिल ।"
"518","I feel much better today.","thauṃ jitaḥ yakva bāṃlāḥgu tāyekā .","थौं जितः यक्व बांलाःगु तायेका ।"
"519","She takes medicine every day.","vaṃ nhiṃ nhiṃ vāsaḥ nai .","वं न्हिं न्हिं वासः नइ ।"
"520","He cannot see without his glasses.","vaṃ casmā madayekaṃ khaṃke phai makhu .","वं चस्मा मदयेकं खंके फै मखु ।"
"521","She has long hair.","vayāke tāḥhākaḥgu saṃ du .","वयाके ताःहाकःगु सँ दु ।"
"522","His eyes are brown.","vayā mikhā siyu .","वया मिखा सियु ।"
"523","My hands are cold.","jigu lhāḥ khvāuṃse cvana .","जिगु ल्हाः ख्वाउँसे च्वन ।"
"524","The child cut his finger.","macāṃ vayāgu patiṃcā tvāḥthala .","मचां वयागु पतिंचा त्वाःथल ।"
"525","I can hear with my ears.","jiṃ jigu nhāypanaṃ nyanephu .","जिं जिगु न्हाय्पनं न्यनेफु ।"
"526","I do not feel well.","jitaḥ mhaṃ maphu .","जितः म्हं मफु ।"
"527","She is not allergic to anything.","vayāta chuṃ naṃ kathaṃyā elarjī madu .","वयात छुं नं कथंया एलर्जी मदु ।"
"528","He did not take his medicine.","vaṃ vayāgu vāsaḥ matvaṃ .","वं वयागु वासः मत्वं ।"
"529","How do you feel?","chanta gathe tāḥ ?","छन्त गथे ताः ?"
"530","Does your head hurt?","chaṃgu chyaṃ syāḥ lā ?","छंगु छ्यं स्याः ला ?"
"531","What happened to your hand?","chaṃgu lhāḥyāta chu jula ?","छंगु ल्हाःयात छु जुल ?"
"532","Have you been to the doctor?","chi ḍākṭarayāthāy jhāyādīgu du lā ?","छि डाक्टरयाथाय् झायादीगु दु ला ?"
"533","When should I take the medicine?","vāsaḥ gubalay nayegu ?","वासः गुबलय् नयेगु ?"
"534","I will see the doctor tomorrow.","jiṃ kanhay ḍākṭarayāta svaye .","जिं कन्हय् डाक्टरयात स्वये ।"
"535","She will recover soon.","va yākanaṃ he lanī .","व याकनं हे लनी ।"
"536","The operation will be performed next week.","megu vālay apareśana yāigu jūgu du .","मेगु वालय् अपरेशन याइगु जूगु दु ।"
"537","I have had a cold for three days.","jitaḥ sardī jūgu svanhu dayedhuṃkala.","जितः सर्दी जूगु स्वन्हु दयेधुंकल।"
"538","She has never been to the hospital.","va gubaleṃ aspatālay mavaṃni .","व गुबलें अस्पतालय् मवंनि ।"
"539","He has already recovered.","vaykaḥ lāyedhuṃkūgu du .","वय्कः लायेधुंकूगु दु ।"
"540","The patient was treated by a specialist.","virāmīyāta viśeṣajñaṃ vāsaḥ yāḥgu khaḥ .","विरामीयात विशेषज्ञं वासः याःगु खः ।"
"541","The medicine must be taken twice a day.","vāsaḥ nhiṃ nikvaḥ naye he māḥ .","वासः न्हिं निक्वः नये हे माः ।"
"542","You should rest.","chaṃ ārāma yāyemāḥ .","छं आराम यायेमाः ।"
"543","I must take care of my health.","jiṃ thaḥgu svāsthyayā bicāḥ yāyemāḥ .","जिं थःगु स्वास्थ्यया बिचाः यायेमाः ।"
"544","She can walk again after the surgery.","śalyakriyā lipā va hānaṃ nyāsi vanephu .","शल्यक्रिया लिपा व हानं न्यासि वनेफु ।"
"545","He might need an operation.","vayāta apareśana yāyemālīphu .","वयात अपरेशन यायेमालीफु ।"
"546","Drink plenty of water.","yakva laḥ tvaṃ .","यक्व लः त्वँ ।"
"547","Do not eat too much sugar.","cini yakva naye mate .","चिनि यक्व नये मते ।"
"548","Take this medicine after meals.","thva vāsaḥ nayedhuṃkāḥ nayegu yā.","थ्व वासः नयेधुंकाः नयेगु या।"
"549","If you exercise regularly, you will be healthier.","niyamita byāyāma yāta dhāḥsā svastha jui.","नियमित ब्यायाम यात धाःसा स्वस्थ जुइ।"
"550","If I had slept more, I would not be so tired.","ji apvaḥ dyanāgu jūsā, ji uli tyānui makhu jui .","जि अप्वः द्यनागु जूसा, जि उलि त्यानुइ मखु जुइ ।"
"551","I feel better than yesterday.","jitaḥ mhigaḥ svayāṃ bāṃlāḥgu tāyekā .","जितः म्हिगः स्वयां बांलाःगु तायेका ।"
"552","She is stronger than she looks.","va svayebalay svayāḥ naṃ ballāḥ .","व स्वयेबलय् स्वयाः नं बल्लाः ।"
"553","This is the worst pain I have ever felt.","thva jiṃ āḥtaka mahasusa yānāgu dakalay bāṃmalāḥgu syāḥcāḥ khaḥ .","थ्व जिं आःतक महसुस यानागु दकलय् बांमलाःगु स्याःचाः खः ।"
"554","Exercise is good for your health.","byāyāma chigu svāsthyayā niṃtiṃ bāṃlāḥ .","ब्यायाम छिगु स्वास्थ्यया निंतिं बांलाः ।"
"555","Smoking is bad for your lungs.","curoṭa tvanegu chigu svaṃyā niṃtiṃ bāṃmalāḥ .","चुरोट त्वनेगु छिगु स्वँया निंतिं बांमलाः ।"
"556","The nurse checked my blood pressure.","narsaṃ jigu raktacāpa jāṃca yāta .","नर्सं जिगु रक्तचाप जाँच यात ।"
"557","I need to get more sleep.","jitaḥ apvaḥ dyanemāḥgu du .","जितः अप्वः द्यनेमाःगु दु ।"
"558","Wash your hands before eating.","naye nhyaḥ lhāḥ silemāḥ .","नये न्ह्यः ल्हाः सिलेमाः ।"
"559","She was born with a heart condition.","va nugaḥcuyā lvacaṃ būgu khaḥ .","व नुगःचुया ल्वचं बूगु खः ।"
"560","He hurt himself while playing.","vaṃ mhitācvaṃbalay thaḥthamhaṃ ghāḥpāḥ yāta .","वं म्हिताच्वंबलय् थःथम्हं घाःपाः यात ।"
"561","My back has been hurting all week.","jigu jaṃ vāḥ nyaṃkaṃ syānācvana .","जिगु जँ वाः न्यंकं स्यानाच्वन ।"
"562","The pain went away after I took the medicine.","vāsaḥ nayedhuṃkāḥ syāḥgu lanāvana .","वासः नयेधुंकाः स्याःगु लनावन ।"
"563","She fainted because she had not eaten.","vaṃ naye makhaṃguliṃ va behosa jula .","वं नये मखंगुलिं व बेहोस जुल ।"
"564","I want to buy a new shirt.","jitaḥ nhūgu kamija nyāye māsti vaḥ .","जितः न्हूगु कमिज न्याये मास्ति वः ।"
"565","She goes shopping every Saturday.","va sanibārapatikaṃ pasaḥ vanī .","व सनिबारपतिकं पसः वनी ।"
"566","He paid for the groceries.","vaṃ kirānā sāmānayā dhyabā pula .","वं किराना सामानया ध्यबा पुल ।"
"567","We need more money.","jhīta apvaḥ dhyabā māla .","झीत अप्वः ध्यबा माल ।"
"568","They sell fruit at the market.","imisaṃ bajāray sisābusā mii .","इमिसं बजारय् सिसाबुसा मिइ ।"
"569","How much does this cost?","thukiyā guli tui ?","थुकिया गुलि तुइ ?"
"570","This is too expensive.","thva tasakaṃ thike .","थ्व तसकं थिके ।"
"571","That dress is very beautiful.","va vasaḥ tasakaṃ bāṃlāḥ .","व वसः तसकं बांलाः ।"
"572","The store is open until nine.","pasaḥ nyātāḥ ī taka cālī .","पसः न्याताः ई तक चाली ।"
"573","I found a great deal.","jiṃ taḥdhaṃgu khaṃ luikā .","जिं तःधंगु खँ लुइका ।"
"574","She bought three pairs of shoes.","vaṃ svajvaḥ lākāṃ nyāta .","वं स्वज्वः लाकां न्यात ।"
"575","He spent all his money.","vaṃ vayāgu phukka dhyabā kharca yāta .","वं वयागु फुक्क ध्यबा खर्च यात ।"
"576","We saved enough money for the trip.","jimisaṃ yātrāyā niṃtiṃ gākka dhyabā muṃkā .","जिमिसं यात्राया निंतिं गाक्क ध्यबा मुंका ।"
"577","I did not buy anything.","jiṃ chuṃ naṃ nyānāgu madu .","जिं छुं नं न्यानागु मदु ।"
"578","They returned the broken item.","imisaṃ syaṃgu sāmāna lita bila .","इमिसं स्यंगु सामान लित बिल ।"
"579","I will go shopping tomorrow.","ji kanhay pasaḥ vane .","जि कन्हय् पसः वने ।"
"580","She is going to buy a gift for her friend.","vaṃ vayā pāsāyāta dvahalapā nyāyetyaṃgu du .","वं वया पासायात द्वहलपा न्यायेत्यंगु दु ।"
"581","The price will go up next month.","megu lāy bhāḥ thahāṃ vanī .","मेगु लाय् भाः थहां वनी ।"
"582","Will you pay by card or cash?","chiṃ kārḍaṃ pule lāki nagadaṃ ?","छिं कार्डं पुले लाकि नगदं ?"
"583","I am looking for a birthday present.","jiṃ janmanhiyā niṃtiṃ dvahalapā mālācvanā .","जिं जन्मन्हिया निंतिं द्वहलपा मालाच्वना ।"
"584","She is trying on a dress.","vaṃ vasaḥ punegu kutaḥ yānācvana .","वं वसः पुनेगु कुतः यानाच्वन ।"
"585","I have already spent too much.","jiṃ yakva kharca yāye dhuna .","जिं यक्व खर्च याये धुन ।"
"586","She has not decided what to buy.","vaṃ chu nyāyegu dhakāḥ kvaḥchī maphuni .","वं छु न्यायेगु धकाः क्वःछी मफुनि ।"
"587","We have been saving for months.","jimisaṃ mahinauṃniseṃ bacata yānācvanāgu du .","जिमिसं महिनाौंनिसें बचत यानाच्वनागु दु ।"
"588","The item was sold out.","sāmāna mī dhuṃkala .","सामान मी धुंकल ।"
"589","Credit cards are accepted here.","thana kreḍiṭa kārḍa svīkāra yāi .","थन क्रेडिट कार्ड स्वीकार याइ ।"
"590","The price has been reduced.","bhāḥ kvakāḥgu du .","भाः क्वकाःगु दु ।"
"591","You can pay at the counter.","chiṃ kāunṭaray dhyabā pulephai .","छिं काउन्टरय् ध्यबा पुलेफइ ।"
"592","I cannot afford this.","jiṃ thva nyāye maphu .","जिं थ्व न्याये मफु ।"
"593","She should save more money.","vaṃ apvaḥ dhyabā muṃke māḥ .","वं अप्वः ध्यबा मुंके माः ।"
"594","May I try this on?","jiṃ thva kutaḥ yāye phai lā ?","जिं थ्व कुतः याये फै ला ?"
"595","Give me the receipt, please.","jitaḥ rasida biyādisaṃ .","जितः रसिद बियादिसँ ।"
"596","Do not forget your change.","chigu hilāsū lvaḥmaṃke mate .","छिगु हिलासू ल्वःमंके मते ।"
"597","If it were cheaper, I would buy it.","thva daṃgu jūsā, jiṃ thva nyāyegu jui .","थ्व दंगु जूसा, जिं थ्व न्यायेगु जुइ ।"
"598","If I had enough money, I would buy a car.","jikey gākka dhyabā dugu khaḥsā, jiṃ chagaḥ kāra nyāyegu jui .","जिकेय् गाक्क ध्यबा दुगु खःसा, जिं छगः कार न्यायेगु जुइ ।"
"599","This store is cheaper than that one.","thva pasaḥ va svayāṃ daṃ .","थ्व पसः व स्वयां दं ।"
"600","The black shoes are more expensive than the brown ones.","siyugu lākāṃ svayāṃ hākugu lākāṃ thike .","सियुगु लाकां स्वयां हाकुगु लाकां थिके ।"
"601","She always finds the best prices.","vaṃ nhyābaleṃ bāṃlāḥgu mū luikī .","वं न्ह्याबलें बांलाःगु मू लुइकी ।"
"602","I bought a few things.","jiṃ chuṃ sāmāna nyānā .","जिं छुं सामान न्याना ।"
"603","She did not buy any clothes.","vaṃ chuṃ naṃ vasaḥ manyā .","वं छुं नं वसः मन्या ।"
"604","Everything in this store is on sale.","thugu pasalay dugu sakatāṃ sāmāna miyācvaṃgu du .","थुगु पसलय् दुगु सकतां सामान मियाच्वंगु दु ।"
"605","None of the items were available.","chuṃ naṃ sāmāna upalabdha majū .","छुं नं सामान उपलब्ध मजू ।"
"606","The store where I bought this is now closed.","jiṃ thva nyānāgu pasaḥ āḥ banda jula .","जिं थ्व न्यानागु पसः आः बन्द जुल ।"
"607","The shirt that he is wearing was a gift.","vaṃ punācvaṃgu kamija dvahalapā khaḥ .","वं पुनाच्वंगु कमिज द्वहलपा खः ।"
"608","She bargained for a lower price.","vaṃ mhva mūyā niṃtiṃ bhāḥ yāta .","वं म्ह्व मूया निंतिं भाः यात ।"
"609","The cashier gave me the wrong change.","kyāsiyaraṃ jitaḥ makhugu hilāsū bila .","क्यासियरं जितः मखुगु हिलासू बिल ।"
"610","I always compare prices before buying.","jiṃ nhyābaleṃ nyāye nhyaḥ mū dānā svaye .","जिं न्ह्याबलें न्याये न्ह्यः मू दाना स्वये ।"
"611","He owes me fifty dollars.","vaṃ jikey nyay ḍalara pulemāḥ .","वं जिकेय् न्यय् डलर पुलेमाः ।"
"612","We split the bill.","jimisaṃ bila bvathalā .","जिमिसं बिल ब्वथला ।"
"613","I prefer quality over quantity.","jitaḥ mātrā svayāṃ guṇastara yaḥ .","जितः मात्रा स्वयां गुणस्तर यः ।"
"614","Money does not grow on trees.","dhyabā simāy buyāvai makhu .","ध्यबा सिमाय् बुयावइ मखु ।"
"615","I am happy.","ji lay tāḥ .","जि लय् ताः ।"
"616","She is sad.","va nugaḥ machiṃ .","व नुगः मछिं ।"
"617","He is angry.","va taṃ cāḥ .","व तं चाः ।"
"618","We are excited.","jipiṃ utsāhita juyācvanā .","जिपिं उत्साहित जुयाच्वना ।"
"619","They are afraid of the dark.","ipiṃ khiuṃ khanāḥ gyāḥ .","इपिं खिउँ खनाः ग्याः ।"
"620","I feel lonely.","jitaḥ yākaḥcā tāyekā .","जितः याकःचा तायेका ।"
"621","She looks worried.","va cintita khanedu .","व चिन्तित खनेदु ।"
"622","He seems tired.","va tyānu theṃ cvaṃ .","व त्यानु थें च्वं ।"
"623","The news made me happy.","bukhaṃṃ jitaḥ laytāyekala .","बुखँं जितः लय्तायेकल ।"
"624","The movie was very sad.","saṃkipā tasakaṃ duḥkhada jula .","संकिपा तसकं दुःखद जुल ।"
"625","I love her.","jiṃ vayāta matinā yānā .","जिं वयात मतिना याना ।"
"626","She misses him.","vaṃ vayāta lumaṃkala .","वं वयात लुमंकल ।"
"627","He trusts his best friend.","vaṃ vayā dakalay mile jūmha pāsāyāta viśvāsa yāi .","वं वया दकलय् मिले जूम्ह पासायात विश्वास याइ ।"
"628","We care about each other.","jimisaṃ thaḥthavay vāstā yānā .","जिमिसं थःथवय् वास्ता याना ।"
"629","They are getting married.","imigu ihipāḥ juityaṃgu du .","इमिगु इहिपाः जुइत्यंगु दु ।"
"630","I met her at a party.","jiṃ vayāta bhvajay nāpa lānā .","जिं वयात भ्वजय् नाप लाना ।"
"631","She fell in love with him.","va valise matinā jula .","व वलिसे मतिना जुल ।"
"632","He asked her to marry him.","vaṃ vayāta thaḥlise ihipāḥ yāyeta dhāla .","वं वयात थःलिसे इहिपाः यायेत धाल ।"
"633","We argued about something silly.","jipiṃ chuṃ mūrkha khaṃy bahasa yānā .","जिपिं छुं मूर्ख खँय् बहस याना ।"
"634","They broke up last year.","ipiṃ thagune brekaapa jula .","इपिं थगुने ब्रेकअप जुल ।"
"635","I am not jealous.","jitaḥ ḍāha madu .","जितः डाह मदु ।"
"636","She does not love him anymore.","vaṃ vayāta āḥ matinā mayāḥ .","वं वयात आः मतिना मयाः ।"
"637","He never gets angry.","va gubaleṃ taṃ cāi makhu .","व गुबलें तं चाइ मखु ।"
"638","We are not fighting.","jipiṃ lvānācvanāgu makhu .","जिपिं ल्वानाच्वनागु मखु ।"
"639","Are you happy?","cha lay tāḥ lā ?","छ लय् ताः ला ?"
"640","Why is she crying?","va chāy khvayācvana ?","व छाय् ख्वयाच्वन ?"
"641","Do you love me?","yāyegu cha matinā jita?","यायेगु छ मतिना जित?"
"642","How long have you been together?","chipiṃ nāpaṃ cvanāgu guli data ?","छिपिं नापं च्वनागु गुलि दत ?"
"643","What makes you happy?","chanta chukiṃ lay tāyekī ?","छन्त छुकिं लय् तायेकी ?"
"644","Who is your best friend?","chimi dakalay mile jūmha pāsā su khaḥ ?","छिमि दकलय् मिले जूम्ह पासा सु खः ?"
"645","I was very happy yesterday.","mhigaḥ ji tasakaṃ lay tāḥgu khaḥ .","म्हिगः जि तसकं लय् ताःगु खः ।"
"646","She cried when she heard the news.","bukhaṃ nyanevaṃ va khvala .","बुखँ न्यनेवं व ख्वल ।"
"647","He apologized for what he said.","vaṃ dhāḥgu khaṃyā niṃtiṃ kṣamā phvana .","वं धाःगु खँया निंतिं क्षमा फ्वन ।"
"648","We forgave each other.","jimisaṃ thaḥthavay kṣamā yānā .","जिमिसं थःथवय् क्षमा याना ।"
"649","They were once very close.","ipiṃ chagū ilay tasakaṃ sattīpiṃ khaḥ .","इपिं छगू इलय् तसकं सत्तीपिं खः ।"
"650","I will always love you.","jiṃ chanta nhyābaleṃ matinā yāye .","जिं छन्त न्ह्याबलें मतिना याये ।"
"651","She will be happy when she hears the news.","bukhaṃ nyanevaṃ va lay tāi .","बुखँ न्यनेवं व लय् ताइ ।"
"652","We will work things out.","jimisaṃ jyā yāye .","जिमिसं ज्या याये ।"
"653","I have known her for many years.","jiṃ vayāta mhasyūgu yakvaṃ dayedhuṃkala .","जिं वयात म्हस्यूगु यक्वं दयेधुंकल ।"
"654","She has always been kind.","va nhyābaleṃ dayālu juyācvana .","व न्ह्याबलें दयालु जुयाच्वन ।"
"655","They have been friends since childhood.","ipiṃ macāṃniseṃ he pāsāpiṃ khaḥ .","इपिं मचांनिसें हे पासापिं खः ।"
"656","If you apologize, she will forgive you.","chaṃ kṣamā phvana dhāḥsā, vaṃ chanta kṣamā yāi .","छं क्षमा फ्वन धाःसा, वं छन्त क्षमा याइ ।"
"657","If I were not so shy, I would talk to her.","ji uli machāḥmha makhusā, jiṃ valise khaṃ lhāyegu jui .","जि उलि मछाःम्ह मखुसा, जिं वलिसे खँ ल्हायेगु जुइ ।"
"658","If he had told the truth, she would not have been angry.","vaṃ khaḥgu khaṃ dhāḥgu jūsā, va taṃ cāi makhu jui .","वं खःगु खँ धाःगु जूसा, व तं चाइ मखु जुइ ।"
"659","You should be more patient.","chaṃ apvaḥ dhairyatā kyanemāḥ .","छं अप्वः धैर्यता क्यनेमाः ।"
"660","I cannot stop thinking about her.","jiṃ vayāgu bāre bicāḥ yāyegu dike maphu .","जिं वयागु बारे बिचाः यायेगु दिके मफु ।"
"661","She must learn to forgive.","vaṃ kṣamā yāyegu sayeke he māḥ .","वं क्षमा यायेगु सयेके हे माः ।"
"662","He was hurt by her words.","vayā khaṃgvalaṃ vayāta nugalay syāta .","वया खँग्वलं वयात नुगलय् स्यात ।"

"668","This is the happiest day of my life.","thva jigu jīvanayā dakalay nhyaipugu nhi khaḥ .","थ्व जिगु जीवनया दकलय् न्ह्यइपुगु न्हि खः ।"

"670","The gift that she gave me means a lot.","vaṃ jitaḥ byūgu dvahalapāyā artha yakva he du .","वं जितः ब्यूगु द्वहलपाया अर्थ यक्व हे दु ।"

"673","I appreciate everything you do.","chaṃ yāigu sakatāṃ jyāyāta jiṃ cvachāyā .","छं याइगु सकतां ज्यायात जिं च्वछाया ।"
"674","She hugged her child tightly.","vaṃ vayā macāyāta kvāttuka ghay pula .","वं वया मचायात क्वात्तुक घय् पुल ।"
"675","Friendship is more important than money.","dhyabā svayāṃ naṃ pāsāpiṃ mahatvapūrṇa jui .","ध्यबा स्वयां नं पासापिं महत्वपूर्ण जुइ ।"
"676","Love is patient and kind.","matinā dhairyavāna va dayālu khaḥ .","मतिना धैर्यवान व दयालु खः ।"
"677","I wish I could see you more often.","jiṃ chanta apvaḥ nāpa lāye phayemā dhakāḥ manaṃtunā .","जिं छन्त अप्वः नाप लाये फयेमा धकाः मनंतुना ।"
"678","They reconciled after a long argument.","tāḥhākaḥgu vādavivāda lipā ipiṃ melamilāpa jula .","ताःहाकःगु वादविवाद लिपा इपिं मेलमिलाप जुल ।"
"679","He regrets what he did.","vaṃ chu yāta dhakāḥ paścātāpa yāta .","वं छु यात धकाः पश्चाताप यात ।"
"680","She confided in her closest friend.","vaṃ vayā dakalay sattīmha pāsāyāta duḥkha pvaṃkala .","वं वया दकलय् सत्तीम्ह पासायात दुःख प्वंकल ।"
