"757","You will take out the trash.","chaṃ phohara pine yaṃkī .","छं फोहर पिने यंकी ।"
"758","Were you sleeping?","cha dyanācvanāgu khaḥ lā ?","छ द्यनाच्वनागु खः ला ?"
"759","I had already gone to bed when they arrived.","ipiṃ thyaṃbalay ji dyane dhuṃkala .","इपिं थ्यंबलय् जि द्यने धुंकल ।"
"760","Have they eaten dinner yet?","imisaṃ āḥtakaṃ beli yāye dhuṃkala lā ?","इमिसं आःतकं बेलि याये धुंकल ला ?"
"761","You should not stay up too late.","cha tasakaṃ libākka taka dane majyū .","छ तसकं लिबाक्क तक दने मज्यू ।"
"762","We could hear the rain from inside.","jimisaṃ duneṃ vā vayācvaṃgu nyanephu .","जिमिसं दुनें वा वयाच्वंगु न्यनेफु ।"
"763","If you go to bed late, you will be tired.","libākka dyaṃsā tyānui .","लिबाक्क द्यंसा त्यानुइ ।"
"764","Do not leave the lights on.","mata cyākāḥ tvaḥte mate .","मत च्याकाः त्वःते मते ।"
"765","I make my bed every morning.","jiṃ nhyābaleṃ suthay jigu lāsā mile yānā .","जिं न्ह्याबलें सुथय् जिगु लासा मिले याना ।"
"766","The laundry has been done.","vasaḥ hiigu jyā sidhala .","वसः हिइगु ज्या सिधल ।"
"767","Nobody answered the phone.","sunānaṃ phona makāla .","सुनानं फोन मकाल ।"
"768","Either you clean up or I will.","ki chaṃ sucukucu yā ki jiṃ yāye .","कि छं सुचुकुचु या कि जिं याये ।"
"769","Whose shoes are these?","thva lākāṃ suyāgu khaḥ ?","थ्व लाकां सुयागु खः ?"
"770","These are not mine.","thupiṃ jigu makhu .","थुपिं जिगु मखु ।"
"771","Each of us has a task.","jhī sakasiyāṃ chagū jyā du .","झी सकसियां छगू ज्या दु ।"
"772","All of the rooms have been cleaned.","dakvaṃ kvathā sucukucu yāye dhuṃkala .","दक्वं क्वथा सुचुकुचु याये धुंकल ।"
"773","I myself cook dinner on Fridays.","jiṃ thaḥmhaṃ he sukrabārakhunhu beli yāye .","जिं थःम्हं हे सुक्रबारखुन्हु बेलि याये ।"
"774","You cooked a wonderful meal.","chaṃ ajū cāyāpugu nasā dayekala .","छं अजू चायापुगु नसा दयेकल ।"
"775","We are eating lunch.","jipiṃ lanca nayācvanā .","जिपिं लन्च नयाच्वना ।"
"776","Were they cooking when you arrived?","cha thyaṃbalay imisaṃ nasā jvare yānācvaṃgu khaḥ lā ?","छ थ्यंबलय् इमिसं नसा ज्वरे यानाच्वंगु खः ला ?"
"777","Will the food be ready by seven?","nhay tāḥ ilay nasā tayāra jui lā ?","न्हय् ताः इलय् नसा तयार जुइ ला ?"
"778","The cake is being baked right now.","āḥ thattheṃ keka beka yānācvaṃgu du .","आः थत्थें केक बेक यानाच्वंगु दु ।"
"779","You should not eat so fast.","chaṃ uli yākanaṃ naye majyū .","छं उलि याकनं नये मज्यू ।"
"780","If she had added more salt, it would have tasted better.","vaṃ ci apvaḥ taḥgu jūsā, thva savāḥ bāṃlāigu khaḥ .","वं चि अप्वः तःगु जूसा, थ्व सवाः बांलाइगु खः ।"
"781","I ate neither the soup nor the salad.","jiṃ na sūpa nayā na salāda .","जिं न सूप नया न सलाद ।"
"782","Each plate has a different design.","pratyeka pleṭayā biskaṃ ḍijāina du .","प्रत्येक प्लेटया बिस्कं डिजाइन दु ।"
"783","These apples are sweeter than those.","thupiṃ syāuta upiṃ svayāṃ cāku .","थुपिं स्याउत उपिं स्वयां चाकु ।"
"784","What kind of food do they eat?","imisaṃ gajyāḥgu nasā nai ?","इमिसं गज्याःगु नसा नइ ?"
"785","We ourselves grew these vegetables.","jimisaṃ thaḥpiṃsaṃ he thva tarakārī buikāgu khaḥ .","जिमिसं थःपिंसं हे थ्व तरकारी बुइकागु खः ।"
"786","Whose plate is this?","thva suyāgu pleṭa khaḥ ?","थ्व सुयागु प्लेट खः ?"
"787","It smells wonderful in here.","thana dune ajū cāyāpugu bāsnā vaḥ .","थन दुने अजू चायापुगु बास्ना वः ।"
"788","The more you cook, the better you get.","chaṃ guli apvaḥ nasā dayekī, uli he bāṃlāi .","छं गुलि अप्वः नसा दयेकी, उलि हे बांलाइ ।"
"789","I would rather have tea than coffee.","jitaḥ kaphī svayāṃ cyā tvane yaḥ .","जितः कफी स्वयां च्या त्वने यः ।"
"790","Somebody left the oven on.","sunānaṃ obhana cyākāḥ tvaḥtābila .","सुनानं ओभन च्याकाः त्वःताबिल ।"
"791","Has anyone tried this dessert?","thva ḍejarṭa sunānaṃ nayāsvala lā ?","थ्व डेजर्ट सुनानं नयास्वल ला ?"
"792","Nothing tastes better than homemade bread.","cheṃy dayekā taḥgu mari svayāṃ bāṃlāḥgu savāḥ chuṃ madu .","छेँय् दयेका तःगु मरि स्वयां बांलाःगु सवाः छुं मदु ।"
"793","You had better not eat that.","chaṃ va manūsā bāṃlāi .","छं व मनूसा बांलाइ ।"
"794","The recipe calls for two cups of flour.","resipii nigū kapa piṭhṭhā māḥ .","रेसिपिइ निगू कप पिठ्ठा माः ।"
"795","We seldom eat out.","jimisaṃ mhva he jaka pine nayegu yānā .","जिमिसं म्ह्व हे जक पिने नयेगु याना ।"
"796","I will have cooked by the time you arrive.","cha thyanībalay taka jiṃ nasā jvare yāye dhuṃkala jui .","छ थ्यनीबलय् तक जिं नसा ज्वरे याये धुंकल जुइ ।"
"797","You traveled by yourself.","chaṃ thamhaṃ he yātrā yāta .","छं थम्हं हे यात्रा यात ।"
"798","We have been traveling for a week.","jipiṃ yātrā yānāgu chavāḥ dayedhuṃkala .","जिपिं यात्रा यानागु छवाः दयेधुंकल ।"
"799","The passengers were asked to remain seated.","yātrutayta cvaneta dhāḥgu khaḥ .","यात्रुतय्त च्वनेत धाःगु खः ।"
"800","I will have arrived by noon.","ji nhinay taka thyane dhuṃkī .","जि न्हिनय् तक थ्यने धुंकी ।"
"801","Were you waiting long?","cha tāḥī taka piyācvanāgu khaḥ lā ?","छ ताःई तक पियाच्वनागु खः ला ?"
"802","She herself carried all the bags.","vaṃ thamhaṃ he dakvaṃ jholā yaṃkala .","वं थम्हं हे दक्वं झोला यंकल ।"
"803","Nobody told us about the delay.","libāḥgu khaṃ jimita sunānaṃ madhāḥ .","लिबाःगु खँ जिमित सुनानं मधाः ।"
"804","Whose suitcase is this?","thva suyāgu suṭakesa khaḥ ?","थ्व सुयागु सुटकेस खः ?"
"805","The train had left before we arrived.","jipiṃ thyane nhyaḥ he rela vanedhuṃkūgu khaḥ .","जिपिं थ्यने न्ह्यः हे रेल वनेधुंकूगु खः ।"
"806","Each passenger must show identification.","pratyeka yātruṃ mhasīkā kyane he māḥ .","प्रत्येक यात्रुं म्हसीका क्यने हे माः ।"
"807","I would rather fly than take the bus.","ji basaṃ gayegu svayāṃ bvayegu he bāṃlāi .","जि बसं गयेगु स्वयां ब्वयेगु हे बांलाइ ।"
"808","You must not take photographs here.","chiṃ thana kipā kāye majyū .","छिं थन किपा काये मज्यू ।"
"809","The road was being repaired.","laṃ marmata juyācvaṃgu khaḥ .","लँ मर्मत जुयाच्वंगु खः ।"
"810","Neither the bus nor the train was on time.","na basa ilay he vala na rela .","न बस इलय् हे वल न रेल ।"
"811","Which platform does the train leave from?","rela gugu pleṭapharmaṃ pihāṃ vanī ?","रेल गुगु प्लेटफर्मं पिहां वनी ?"
"812","Either we walk or we take a taxi.","ki jipiṃ nyāsi vanegu ki ṭyāksi kāyegu .","कि जिपिं न्यासि वनेगु कि ट्याक्सि कायेगु ।"
"813","He had never flown before that trip.","va yātrā nhyaḥ vaṃ gubaleṃ bvaye makhaṃni .","व यात्रा न्ह्यः वं गुबलें ब्वये मखंनि ।"
"814","How far is the airport from here?","thanaṃ eyaraporṭa guli tāpāḥ ?","थनं एयरपोर्ट गुलि तापाः ?"
"815","The sooner we leave, the earlier we arrive.","jipiṃ guli yākanaṃ vanegu khaḥ, uli he yākanaṃ thyanī .","जिपिं गुलि याकनं वनेगु खः, उलि हे याकनं थ्यनी ।"
"816","I was told to wait here.","jitaḥ thana piyācvaṃ dhāla .","जितः थन पियाच्वँ धाल ।"
"817","You have a beautiful family.","chimike bāṃlāḥgu parivāra du .","छिमिके बांलाःगु परिवार दु ।"
"818","They are building a new house.","nhūgu cheṃ dayekācvaṃgu du .","न्हूगु छेँ दयेकाच्वंगु दु ।"
"819","Were your parents born in this country?","chimi māṃabu thugu desay būgu khaḥ lā ?","छिमि मांअबु थुगु देसय् बूगु खः ला ?"
"820","I will have lived here for twenty years by then.","abalay taka ji thana nīdaṃ cvanāgu jui .","अबलय् तक जि थन नीदँ च्वनागु जुइ ।"
"821","Nobody in our family smokes.","jimi cheṃjalay sunāṃ naṃ curoṭa tvanī makhu .","जिमि छेँजलय् सुनां नं चुरोट त्वनी मखु ।"
"822","Both my parents work.","jimi māṃabu nimhaṃ jyā yāi .","जिमि मांअबु निम्हं ज्या याइ ।"
"823","Neither my brother nor my sister lives nearby.","jimi kijā na jimi keheṃ likka cvanī makhu .","जिमि किजा न जिमि केहें लिक्क च्वनी मखु ।"
"824","Each child has their own room.","pratyeka macāyā thaḥgu he kvathā du .","प्रत्येक मचाया थःगु हे क्वथा दु ।"
"825","Whose toys are these?","thva suyāgu mhibaḥsā khaḥ ?","थ्व सुयागु म्हिबःसा खः ?"
"826","It was my grandmother who taught me to cook.","jitaḥ nasā jvare syanādīmha jimi ajiṃ he khaḥ .","जितः नसा ज्वरे स्यनादीम्ह जिमि अजिं हे खः ।"
"827","My parents had already gone to bed when I came home.","ji cheṃy vayābalay jimi māṃabupiṃ dyanedhuṃkūgu khaḥ .","जि छेँय् वयाबलय् जिमि मांअबुपिं द्यनेधुंकूगु खः ।"
"828","We ourselves built this fence.","thva bāra jimisaṃ he dayekāgu khaḥ .","थ्व बार जिमिसं हे दयेकागु खः ।"
"829","He is taller than any of his brothers.","va vayā suṃ naṃ dājukijā svayāṃ taḥdhikaḥ .","व वया सुं नं दाजुकिजा स्वयां तःधिकः ।"
"830","I am the only child in my family.","jimi cheṃjalay ji chamha jaka macā khaḥ .","जिमि छेँजलय् जि छम्ह जक मचा खः ।"
"831","You should spend more time with your family.","chiṃ chimi cheṃjaḥlise apvaḥ ī chyāyemāḥ .","छिं छिमि छेँजःलिसे अप्वः ई छ्यायेमाः ।"
"832","Had they moved before the baby was born?","macā bui nhyaḥ he ipiṃ sare jūgu khaḥ lā ?","मचा बुइ न्ह्यः हे इपिं सरे जूगु खः ला ?"
"833","The house is being renovated.","cheṃ jīrṇoddhāra juyācvaṃgu du .","छेँ जीर्णोद्धार जुयाच्वंगु दु ।"
"834","I wish my family lived closer.","jimi cheṃjaḥ sattika cvanemā dhakāḥ manaṃtunā .","जिमि छेँजः सत्तिक च्वनेमा धकाः मनंतुना ।"
"835","It is my mother who cooks the best meals.","dakalay bāṃlāḥgu nasā jvare yāimha jimi māṃ khaḥ .","दकलय् बांलाःगु नसा ज्वरे याइम्ह जिमि मां खः ।"
"836","You need to study harder.","chaṃ ajha mehanata yānāḥ bvanemāḥgu du .","छं अझ मेहनत यानाः ब्वनेमाःगु दु ।"
"837","We are being trained by the manager.","jimita myānejaraṃ tālima biyācvana .","जिमित म्यानेजरं तालिम बियाच्वन ।"
"838","Had she finished the project before the deadline?","vaṃ myāda sidhaye nhyaḥ he projekṭa sidhayekūgu khaḥ lā ?","वं म्याद सिधये न्ह्यः हे प्रोजेक्ट सिधयेकूगु खः ला ?"
"839","The exam will have been graded by Monday.","somabārataka jāṃcayā greḍa bīdhuṃkī .","सोमबारतक जाँचया ग्रेड बीधुंकी ।"
"840","Neither the teacher nor the students understood.","na guruṃ thuikala na bvanāmitaseṃ .","न गुरुं थुइकल न ब्वनामितसें ।"
"841","Each student must write an essay.","bvanāmi pattikaṃ cvakhaṃ cvaye he māḥ .","ब्वनामि पत्तिकं च्वखँ च्वये हे माः ।"
"842","Whose pen is this?","thva suyāgu kalama khaḥ ?","थ्व सुयागु कलम खः ?"
"843","It was the teacher who explained the problem.","samasyā thuikā biumha guru he khaḥ .","समस्या थुइका बिउम्ह गुरु हे खः ।"
"844","I myself completed the entire project.","jiṃ thamhaṃ he dakvaṃ pariyojanā pūvaṃkā .","जिं थम्हं हे दक्वं परियोजना पूवंका ।"
"845","Nobody passed the exam.","jāṃcay suṃ naṃ pāsa majū .","जाँचय् सुं नं पास मजू ।"
"846","Were you working when I called?","jiṃ phona yānābalay cha jyā yānācvanāgu khaḥ lā ?","जिं फोन यानाबलय् छ ज्या यानाच्वनागु खः ला ?"
"847","Both the students and the teacher are responsible.","bvanāmi va guru nimhaṃ jimmevāra khaḥ .","ब्वनामि व गुरु निम्हं जिम्मेवार खः ।"
"848","You had better finish your work.","chaṃ chaṃgu jyā sidhayekūsā bāṃlāi .","छं छंगु ज्या सिधयेकूसा बांलाइ ।"
"849","Either she resigns or she is fired.","ki vaṃ rājīnāmā bī ki vayāta likāi .","कि वं राजीनामा बी कि वयात लिकाइ ।"
"850","The harder you study, the higher your grades will be.","chaṃ guli mehanata yānāḥ bvanī, uli he chaṃgu greḍa apvai .","छं गुलि मेहनत यानाः ब्वनी, उलि हे छंगु ग्रेड अप्वइ ।"
"851","I would rather work from home.","jiṃ cheṃy cvanāḥ jyā yāyegu he bāṃlāi .","जिं छेँय् च्वनाः ज्या यायेगु हे बांलाइ ।"
"852","I have not been given a raise.","jitaḥ talaba apvayekūgu maduni .","जितः तलब अप्वयेकूगु मदुनि ।"
"853","Someone forgot their laptop.","suṃ chamhesyāṃ thaḥgu lāy pa ṭapa lvaḥmaṃkala .","सुं छम्हेस्यां थःगु लाय् प टप ल्वःमंकल ।"
"854","Whatever you decide, I will support you.","chaṃ nhyāgu he nirṇaya yāḥsāṃ jiṃ chanta sātha bī .","छं न्ह्यागु हे निर्णय याःसां जिं छन्त साथ बी ।"
"855","She asked whether the meeting had been canceled.","vaṃ muṃjyā radda jūgu khaḥ lā dhakāḥ nyana .","वं मुँज्या रद्द जूगु खः ला धकाः न्यन ।"
"856","You saw the eclipse.","chaṃ grahaṇa khaṃkala .","छं ग्रहण खंकल ।"
"857","We have been watching the storm.","jimisaṃ vāphay vayācvaṃgu svayācvanāgu du .","जिमिसं वाफय् वयाच्वंगु स्वयाच्वनागु दु ।"
"858","Were the animals frightened by the thunder?","janāvarata gaḍagaḍāghaṭaṃ gyāta lā ?","जनावरत गडगडाघटं ग्यात ला ?"
"859","The crops will have been harvested by October.","akṭobara taka bāli laye dhuṃkī .","अक्टोबर तक बालि लये धुंकी ।"
"860","Neither the heat nor the cold bothers me.","na ta tāṃnvaḥ na khvāuṃse jita: dikka du .","न त तांन्वः न ख्वाउँसे जित: दिक्क दु ।"
"861","Each season has its own beauty.","pratyeka mausamayā thaḥgu he sundaratā dai .","प्रत्येक मौसमया थःगु हे सुन्दरता दइ ।"
"862","Whose land is this?","thva suyāgu jaggā?","थ्व सुयागु जग्गा?"
"863","It was the earthquake that destroyed the bridge.","bhukhācaṃ he tāṃ syaṃkūgu khaḥ .","भुखाचं हे तां स्यंकूगु खः ।"
"864","We ourselves planted these trees.","thva simā jimisaṃ he pināgu khaḥ .","थ्व सिमा जिमिसं हे पिनागु खः ।"
"865","Nobody expected the flood.","khusibāḥ vai dhakāḥ sunānaṃ āśā mayāḥgu khaḥ .","खुसिबाः वइ धकाः सुनानं आशा मयाःगु खः ।"
"866","Both the river and the lake are polluted.","khusi va pukhū niguliṃ pradūṣita juyācvaṃgu du .","खुसि व पुखू निगुलिं प्रदूषित जुयाच्वंगु दु ।"
"867","Either it rains or it stays dry.","ki vā vai ki gaṃkā cvanī .","कि वा वइ कि गंका च्वनी ।"
"868","The longer it rains, the higher the river rises.","guli tāḥhākaḥ vā vai, uli he khusi taḥjāḥ jujuṃ vanī .","गुलि ताःहाकः वा वइ, उलि हे खुसि तःजाः जुजुं वनी ।"
"869","I would rather have sunshine.","jitaḥ nibhāḥ daigu he bāṃlāi .","जितः निभाः दइगु हे बांलाइ ।"
"870","You had better bring warm clothes.","chaṃ lumugu vasaḥ haḥsā bāṃlāi .","छं लुमुगु वसः हःसा बांलाइ ।"
"871","Whatever happens, nature will recover.","nhyāgu he jūsāṃ prakr̥tiṃ rikabharī yāi .","न्ह्यागु हे जूसां प्रकृतिं रिकभरी याइ ।"
"872","The forest has been burning for days.","nhinhiniseṃ jaṃgala cyānācvaṃgu du .","न्हिन्हिनिसें जंगल च्यानाच्वंगु दु ।"
"873","Had the volcano erupted before?","jvālāmukhī nhāpā cyāḥgu khaḥ lā ?","ज्वालामुखी न्हापा च्याःगु खः ला ?"
"874","It is being investigated whether the water is safe.","laḥ surakṣita du ki madu dhaigu anusandhāna juyācvaṃgu du .","लः सुरक्षित दु कि मदु धैगु अनुसन्धान जुयाच्वंगु दु ।"
"875","The plants need to be watered.","svāṃmāyāta laḥ tvaṃke māḥ .","स्वांमायात लः त्वंके माः ।"
"876","Somebody must have seen the bear.","sunānaṃ bhāluyāta khaṃgu juimāḥ .","सुनानं भालुयात खंगु जुइमाः ।"
"877","It seldom snows in this region.","thugu lāgāy gubaleṃ gubaleṃ jaka cvāpu gāi .","थुगु लागाय् गुबलें गुबलें जक च्वापु गाइ ।"
"878","I wish it were warmer.","thva lumuse cvaṃsā jyū .","थ्व लुमुसे च्वंसा ज्यू ।"
"879","How deep is the lake?","pukhū guli tajāḥ ?","पुखू गुलि तजाः ?"
"880","The sky turned red at sunset.","nibhāḥ bībalay sargaḥ hyāuṃse cvana .","निभाः बीबलय् सर्गः ह्याउँसे च्वन ।"
"881","Flowers are watered by the gardener every morning.","nhyābaleṃ suthay mālīṃ svāṃyāta laḥ tvaṃkī .","न्ह्याबलें सुथय् मालीं स्वांयात लः त्वंकी ।"
"882","You look pale.","cha khiuṃse cvaṃ .","छ खिउँसे च्वं ।"
"883","We were examined by the specialist.","jimita viśeṣajñaṃ jāṃca yāta .","जिमित विशेषज्ञं जाँच यात ।"
"884","Had he taken his medicine before the attack?","vaṃ ākramaṇa jui nhyaḥ vāsaḥ naḥgu khaḥ lā ?","वं आक्रमण जुइ न्ह्यः वासः नःगु खः ला ?"
"885","The surgery will have been completed by noon.","nhinasiyā dune śalyakriyā kvacāi .","न्हिनसिया दुने शल्यक्रिया क्वचाइ ।"
"886","Neither the doctor nor the nurse was available.","na ḍākṭara du, na narsa .","न डाक्टर दु, न नर्स ।"
"887","Each patient needs individual attention.","pratyeka lvagiyāta byaktigata dhyāna māḥ .","प्रत्येक ल्वगियात ब्यक्तिगत ध्यान माः ।"
"888","Whose prescription is this?","thva suyāgu parcā khaḥ ?","थ्व सुयागु पर्चा खः ?"
"889","It was the infection that caused the fever.","saṃkramaṇayā huniṃ jvara vaḥgu khaḥ .","संक्रमणया हुनिं ज्वर वःगु खः ।"
"890","I myself changed the bandage.","jiṃ thamhaṃ he paṭṭī hilā .","जिं थम्हं हे पट्टी हिला ।"
"891","Nobody heard him call for help.","vaṃ gvāhāliyā niṃtiṃ saḥtūgu sunānaṃ matāḥ .","वं ग्वाहालिया निंतिं सःतूगु सुनानं मताः ।"
"892","Both his arms and his legs were broken.","vayā nipā lhāḥ va tuti tvāḥdala .","वया निपा ल्हाः व तुति त्वाःदल ।"
"893","Either you rest or you will get worse.","ki cha ārāma yāi ki cha jhana bāṃmalānā vanī .","कि छ आराम याइ कि छ झन बांमलाना वनी ।"
"894","The sooner you see a doctor, the better.","guli yākanaṃ chaṃ ḍākṭarayāta svala, uli he bāṃlāi .","गुलि याकनं छं डाक्टरयात स्वल, उलि हे बांलाइ ।"
"895","I would rather avoid surgery.","jiṃ baru sarjarī yāyegupākheṃ bace jui .","जिं बरु सर्जरी यायेगुपाखें बचे जुइ ।"
"896","You had better take this seriously.","chaṃ thukiyāta gambhīrakathaṃ kāyegu bāṃlāi .","छं थुकियात गम्भीरकथं कायेगु बांलाइ ।"
"897","Whatever the diagnosis, we will face it together.","nhyāgu he nidāna jūsāṃ jhīsaṃ nāpaṃ sāmanā yāye .","न्ह्यागु हे निदान जूसां झीसं नापं सामना याये ।"
"898","The wound is being treated.","ghāḥyā vāsaḥ juyācvaṃgu du .","घाःया वासः जुयाच्वंगु दु ।"
"899","Someone called an ambulance.","sunānaṃ embulensa saḥtala .","सुनानं एम्बुलेन्स सःतल ।"
"900","I wish I had gone to the doctor sooner.","ji yākanaṃ ḍākṭarayāthāy vanāgu jūsā jyū .","जि याकनं डाक्टरयाथाय् वनागु जूसा ज्यू ।"
"901","How long has she been in the hospital?","va aspatālay guli data ?","व अस्पतालय् गुलि दत ?"
"902","The patient is being monitored closely.","virāmīyāta satarkatāpūrvaka anugamana yānācvaṃgu du .","विरामीयात सतर्कतापूर्वक अनुगमन यानाच्वंगु दु ।"
"903","His condition has improved significantly.","vaykaḥyā avasthāy ullekhanīya sudhāra jūgu du .","वय्कःया अवस्थाय् उल्लेखनीय सुधार जूगु दु ।"
"904","She is too weak to walk.","va nyāsi vaneta tasakaṃ bamalāḥ .","व न्यासि वनेत तसकं बमलाः ।"
"905","The doctor asked him to lie down.","ḍākṭaraṃ vayāta gvārtu dhāla .","डाक्टरं वयात ग्वार्तु धाल ।"
"906","I rarely get sick.","ji mhva he jaka mhaṃ maphu .","जि म्ह्व हे जक म्हं मफु ।"
"907","Regular exercise keeps you healthy.","niyamita byāyāmaṃ chanta svastha yānātai .","नियमित ब्यायामं छन्त स्वस्थ यानातइ ।"
"908","You paid too much for that.","ukiyā niṃtiṃ chaṃ yakva dhyabā pula .","उकिया निंतिं छं यक्व ध्यबा पुल ।"
"909","We are being overcharged.","jimita apvaḥ dhyabā kayācvaṃgu du .","जिमित अप्वः ध्यबा कयाच्वंगु दु ।"
"910","Had they already closed when you arrived?","cha thyaṃbalay imisaṃ banda yāye dhuṃkala lā ?","छ थ्यंबलय् इमिसं बन्द याये धुंकल ला ?"
"911","The order will have been delivered by Friday.","arḍara śukrabārataka thyaṃke dhuṃkī .","अर्डर शुक्रबारतक थ्यंके धुंकी ।"
"912","Neither the red dress nor the blue one fits me.","na hyāuṃgu vasaḥ jitaḥ pāy chi majū, na vaṃcugu .","न ह्याउँगु वसः जितः पाय् छि मजू, न वँचुगु ।"
"913","Each item has a different price.","pratyeka sāmānayā mū biskaṃ biskaṃ jui .","प्रत्येक सामानया मू बिस्कं बिस्कं जुइ ।"
"914","Whose wallet is this?","thva suyāgu vāleṭa khaḥ ?","थ्व सुयागु वालेट खः ?"
"915","It was the manager who helped me.","jitaḥ gvāhāli yāḥmha myānejara he khaḥ .","जितः ग्वाहालि याःम्ह म्यानेजर हे खः ।"
"916","I myself wrapped all the gifts.","jiṃ thamhaṃ he dakvaṃ dvahalapā pvaḥcinā .","जिं थम्हं हे दक्वं द्वहलपा प्वःचिना ।"
"917","Nobody bought the old furniture.","pulāṃgu pharnicara sunānaṃ nyāḥgu madu .","पुलांगु फर्निचर सुनानं न्याःगु मदु ।"
"918","Both the quality and the price are good.","guṇastara va mū nittāṃ bāṃlāḥ .","गुणस्तर व मू नित्तां बांलाः ।"
"919","Either I buy it now or someone else will.","ki jiṃ āḥ nyāye ki memhasyāṃ nyāye .","कि जिं आः न्याये कि मेम्हस्यां न्याये ।"
"920","The more you buy, the more you save.","guli apvaḥ nyāta, uli he apvaḥ bacata jui .","गुलि अप्वः न्यात, उलि हे अप्वः बचत जुइ ।"
"921","I would rather save the money.","jiṃ dhyabā muṃkegu he bāṃlāi .","जिं ध्यबा मुंकेगु हे बांलाइ ।"
"922","You had better check your receipt.","chaṃ chaṃgu rasida jāṃca yāḥsā bāṃlāi .","छं छंगु रसिद जाँच याःसा बांलाइ ।"
"923","Whatever she buys, she always returns it.","vaṃ nhyāgu nyāḥsāṃ nhyābaleṃ lita bī .","वं न्ह्यागु न्याःसां न्ह्याबलें लित बी ।"
"924","The product is being recalled.","utpādanayāta lita hayetyaṃgu du .","उत्पादनयात लित हयेत्यंगु दु ।"
"925","Someone stole my wallet.","sunānaṃ jigu vāleṭa khuyā yaṃkala .","सुनानं जिगु वालेट खुया यंकल ।"
"926","I wish it were cheaper.","thva daṃgu jūsā jyū dhakāḥ manaṃtunā .","थ्व दंगु जूसा ज्यू धकाः मनंतुना ।"
"927","How much do we owe?","jhīke guli pulemāḥgu du ?","झीके गुलि पुलेमाःगु दु ?"
"928","They themselves made this product.","imisaṃ thaḥthamhaṃ he thugu utpādana dayekūgu khaḥ .","इमिसं थःथम्हं हे थुगु उत्पादन दयेकूगु खः ।"
"929","Hardly anyone shops there.","ana sāyada he sunāṃ naṃ pasaḥ tai .","अन सायद हे सुनां नं पसः तइ ।"
"930","You seem upset.","cha mhāipuse cvaṃ .","छ म्हाइपुसे च्वं ।"
"931","We are being ignored.","jimita bevāstā yānācvaṃgu du .","जिमित बेवास्ता यानाच्वंगु दु ।"
"932","Had they spoken to each other before the meeting?","imisaṃ muṃjyā nhyaḥ thaḥthavay khaṃ lhāḥgu khaḥ lā ?","इमिसं मुँज्या न्ह्यः थःथवय् खँ ल्हाःगु खः ला ?"
"933","The issue will have been resolved by tomorrow.","kanhay takayā dune thva khaṃ jyanīgu jui .","कन्हय् तकया दुने थ्व खँ ज्यनीगु जुइ ।"
"934","Neither he nor she wanted to apologize.","na vaṃ na vaṃ māphī phvane māsti vaḥ .","न वं न वं माफी फ्वने मास्ति वः ।"
"935","Each person reacted differently.","pratyeka manukhaṃ biskaṃ biskaṃ pratikriyā bila .","प्रत्येक मनुखं बिस्कं बिस्कं प्रतिक्रिया बिल ।"
"936","It was she who forgave first.","nhāpāṃ kṣamā yāḥmha va he khaḥ .","न्हापां क्षमा याःम्ह व हे खः ।"
"937","I myself do not understand my own feelings.","jiṃ thaḥgu he bhāvanā thuike maphu .","जिं थःगु हे भावना थुइके मफु ।"
"938","Nobody expected the surprise.","sunānaṃ ajūcāyāpugu āśā mayāḥgu khaḥ .","सुनानं अजूचायापुगु आशा मयाःगु खः ।"
"939","Both happiness and sadness are part of life.","sukha va duḥkha nittāṃ jīvanayā bva khaḥ .","सुख व दुःख नित्तां जीवनया ब्व खः ।"
"940","Either we talk or the problem gets worse.","ki jhīsaṃ khaṃ lhāyegu ki samasyā jhana jhana bāṃmalānā vanī .","कि झीसं खँ ल्हायेगु कि समस्या झन झन बांमलाना वनी ।"
"941","The longer you wait, the harder it becomes.","guli tāḥī taka piyācvanī, uli he thākuyā vai .","गुलि ताःई तक पियाच्वनी, उलि हे थाकुया वइ ।"
"942","I would rather be alone right now.","ji āḥ he yākaḥcā juigu he bāṃlāi .","जि आः हे याकःचा जुइगु हे बांलाइ ।"
"943","You had better apologize.","chaṃ kṣamā phvaṃgu bāṃlāi .","छं क्षमा फ्वंगु बांलाइ ।"
"944","Whatever happens between us, I will remember you.","jhī dathui nhyāgu he jūsāṃ jiṃ chanta lumaṃke .","झी दथुइ न्ह्यागु हे जूसां जिं छन्त लुमंके ।"
"945","She had been crying before I arrived.","ji thyane nhyaḥ he va khvayācvana .","जि थ्यने न्ह्यः हे व ख्वयाच्वन ।"
"946","Someone must have told her.","sunānaṃ vayāta dhāḥgu juimāḥ .","सुनानं वयात धाःगु जुइमाः ।"
"947","I wish we had met sooner.","jipiṃ yākanaṃ nāpa lāḥgu jūsā jyū dhakāḥ manaṃtunā .","जिपिं याकनं नाप लाःगु जूसा ज्यू धकाः मनंतुना ।"
"948","How do you cope with stress?","chaṃ tanāvayāta gathe yānāḥ sāmanā yāyegu ?","छं तनावयात गथे यानाः सामना यायेगु ?"
"949","Hardly anyone noticed his sadness.","vayāgu duḥkha sāyada he sunānaṃ vāḥ cāyekala .","वयागु दुःख सायद हे सुनानं वाः चायेकल ।"
"950","They themselves decided to separate.","imisaṃ thaḥpiṃsaṃ he alaga juigu nirṇaya yāta .","इमिसं थःपिंसं हे अलग जुइगु निर्णय यात ।"
"951","It is believed that love conquers all.","matināṃ sakasitaṃ tyākī dhaigu viśvāsa du .","मतिनां सकसितं त्याकी धइगु विश्वास दु ।"
"952","Were you surprised by the news?","bukhaṃ nyanāḥ cha ajū cāla lā ?","बुखँ न्यनाः छ अजू चाल ला ?"
"953","You played very well.","cha tasakaṃ bāṃlāka mhitala .","छ तसकं बांलाक म्हितल ।"
"954","We are being entertained by a magician.","jimita chamha jādugaraṃ nhyaipukā cvana .","जिमित छम्ह जादुगरं न्ह्यइपुका च्वन ।"
"955","Had she practiced before the performance?","vaṃ pyākhaṃ nhyabvaye nhyaḥ abhyāsa yāḥgu khaḥ lā ?","वं प्याखं न्ह्यब्वये न्ह्यः अभ्यास याःगु खः ला ?"
"956","The movie will have started by the time we arrive.","jipiṃ thyanībalay taka saṃkipā nhyāye dhuṃkī .","जिपिं थ्यनीबलय् तक संकिपा न्ह्याये धुंकी ।"
"957","Neither the book nor the movie was interesting.","na saphū na saṃkipā nhyaipuse cvaṃ .","न सफू न संकिपा न्ह्यइपुसे च्वं ।"
"958","Each player received a medal.","pratyeka kāsāmiṃ chagū padaka laḥlhāta .","प्रत्येक कासामिं छगू पदक लःल्हात ।"
"959","Whose guitar is this?","thva suyāgu gitāra khaḥ ?","थ्व सुयागु गितार खः ?"
"960","It was the goal that won the match.","thva golaṃ he kāsā tyākūgu khaḥ .","थ्व गोलं हे कासा त्याकूगु खः ।"
"961","I myself composed this song.","thva mye ji thamhaṃ he saṃgīta tayāgu khaḥ .","थ्व म्ये जि थम्हं हे संगीत तयागु खः ।"
"962","Nobody scored in the first half.","nhāpāṃgu hāphay sunāṃ naṃ gola mayāḥgu khaḥ .","न्हापांगु हाफय् सुनां नं गोल मयाःगु खः ।"
"963","Both the actors and the director were praised.","kalākāra va nirdeśaka nimhesyāṃ he cvachāḥgu khaḥ .","कलाकार व निर्देशक निम्हेस्यां हे च्वछाःगु खः ।"
"964","Either we win or we learn.","ki jhī tyāi ki jhīsaṃ sayeke .","कि झी त्याइ कि झीसं सयेके ।"
"965","The more you practice, the better you play.","guli apvaḥ abhyāsa yāi, uli he bāṃlāka mhitī .","गुलि अप्वः अभ्यास याइ, उलि हे बांलाक म्हिती ।"
"966","I would rather watch a comedy.","jiṃ baru khyālaḥ svayegu he bāṃlāi .","जिं बरु ख्यालः स्वयेगु हे बांलाइ ।"
"967","You had better not miss the show.","chaṃ śo tvaḥphike maphugu bāṃlāi .","छं शो त्वःफिके मफुगु बांलाइ ।"
"968","Whatever you choose, it will be fun.","chaṃ nhyāgu he lyaḥsāṃ, thva nhyaipui .","छं न्ह्यागु हे ल्यःसां, थ्व न्ह्यइपुइ ।"
"969","The show had been sold out for weeks.","thugu śo chuṃ vāḥniseṃ phukkaṃ mīdhuṃkūgu khaḥ .","थुगु शो छुं वाःनिसें फुक्कं मीधुंकूगु खः ।"
"970","Someone left their jacket at the theater.","suṃ chamhesyāṃ imigu jyākeṭa dabulii tvaḥtāḥ vana .","सुं छम्हेस्यां इमिगु ज्याकेट दबुलिइ त्वःताः वन ।"
"971","I wish I could play an instrument.","jiṃ bājaṃ thāye phayemā dhakāḥ manaṃtunā .","जिं बाजं थाये फयेमा धकाः मनंतुना ।"
"972","How many people attended the festival?","mahotsavay gvaḥmha manūta upasthita jula ?","महोत्सवय् ग्वःम्ह मनूत उपस्थित जुल ?"
"973","We ourselves organized the event.","jyājhvaḥ jimisaṃ he gvasāḥ gvayāgu khaḥ .","ज्याझ्वः जिमिसं हे ग्वसाः ग्वयागु खः ।"
"974","Hardly anyone came to the recital.","pāṭhay sāyada he suṃ naṃ mavaḥ .","पाठय् सायद हे सुं नं मवः ।"
"975","It is said that he is the most talented musician.","vaykaḥ dakalay pratibhāśālī saṃgītakaḥmi khaḥ dhāi .","वय्कः दकलय् प्रतिभाशाली संगीतकःमि खः धाइ ।"
"976","Were the children allowed to participate?","mastay ta bvati kāyeta anumati byūgu khaḥ lā ?","मस्तय् त ब्वति कायेत अनुमति ब्यूगु खः ला ?"
"977","The crowd cheered as the team entered the field.","ṭima maidānay duhāṃ vayevaṃ bhīḍaṃ jayajayakāra yāta .","टिम मैदानय् दुहां वयेवं भीडं जयजयकार यात ।"
"978","He is too young to enter this club.","thva klabay duhāṃ vaneta va tasakaṃ lyāy mha .","थ्व क्लबय् दुहां वनेत व तसकं ल्याय् म्ह ।"
"979","I speak two languages.","jiṃ nigū bhāy lhāye saḥ .","जिं निगू भाय् ल्हाये सः ।"
"980","She writes letters to her friends.","vaṃ vayā pāsāpinta pau cvai .","वं वया पासापिन्त पौ च्वइ ।"
"981","He calls his mother every Sunday.","vaṃ vayā māṃyāta āitabārapatikaṃ phona yāi .","वं वया मांयात आइतबारपतिकं फोन याइ ।"
"982","We chat online every evening.","jipiṃ nhyābaleṃ saniilay analāina khaṃlhābalhā yānā .","जिपिं न्ह्याबलें सनिइलय् अनलाइन खँल्हाबल्हा याना ।"
"983","They greet each other warmly.","imisaṃ thaḥthavay lumuka abhivādana yāta .","इमिसं थःथवय् लुमुक अभिवादन यात ।"
"984","I sent her an email.","jiṃ vayāta imela chvayā .","जिं वयात इमेल छ्वया ।"
"985","She received a message from him.","vaṃ vayāpākheṃ hasanā vala .","वं वयापाखें हसना वल ।"
"986","He introduced himself to everyone.","vaṃ sakasitaṃ thaḥgu mhasīkā bila .","वं सकसितं थःगु म्हसीका बिल ।"
"987","We have been friends for years.","jipiṃ daṃ daṃniseṃ pāsāpiṃ khaḥ .","जिपिं दँ दँनिसें पासापिं खः ।"
"988","They promised to help us.","imisaṃ jimita gvāhāli yāyegu bacaṃ bila .","इमिसं जिमित ग्वाहालि यायेगु बचं बिल ।"
"989","I do not understand what he is saying.","vaṃ chu dhayācvana jiṃ mathula .","वं छु धयाच्वन जिं मथुल ।"
"990","She did not reply to my message.","vaṃ jigu hasanāyā lisaḥ mabyū .","वं जिगु हसनाया लिसः मब्यू ।"
"991","He never lies.","vaṃ gubaleṃ makhugu khaṃ lhāi makhu .","वं गुबलें मखुगु खँ ल्हाइ मखु ।"
"992","We have not spoken in months.","jimisaṃ mahinauṃniseṃ khaṃ malhānāni .","जिमिसं महिनाौंनिसें खँ मल्हानानि ।"
"993","Do you speak English?","chaṃ aṃgreji bhāy lhāye saḥ lā ?","छं अंग्रेजि भाय् ल्हाये सः ला ?"
"994","What did she say?","vaṃ chu dhāla ?","वं छु धाल ?"
"995","Who called you?","chanta sunāṃ saḥtala ?","छन्त सुनां सःतल ?"
"996","Can you translate this for me?","chiṃ jigu niṃtiṃ thva bhāy hilādī phai lā ?","छिं जिगु निंतिं थ्व भाय् हिलादी फै ला ?"
"997","How do you say this in your language?","thva chigu bhāsaṃ gathe yānāḥ dhāyegu ?","थ्व छिगु भासं गथे यानाः धायेगु ?"
"998","Have you heard the news?","chaṃ bukhaṃ nyanāgu du lā ?","छं बुखँ न्यनागु दु ला ?"
"999","I will call you later.","jiṃ chanta lipā kala yāye .","जिं छन्त लिपा कल याये ।"
"1000","She is going to give a speech.","vaṃ bhāṣaṇa bītyaṃgu du .","वं भाषण बीत्यंगु दु ।"
"1001","We will discuss it tomorrow.","jhīsaṃ kanhay sahalaha yāye .","झीसं कन्हय् सहलह याये ।"
"1002","Will they announce the results?","imisaṃ licvaḥ ghoṣaṇā yāi lā ?","इमिसं लिच्वः घोषणा याइ ला ?"
"1003","I was talking to her when you interrupted.","chaṃ paṃgaḥ thanabalay ji valise khaṃ lhānācvanāgu khaḥ .","छं पंगः थनबलय् जि वलिसे खँ ल्हानाच्वनागु खः ।"
"1004","She is explaining the rules.","vaṃ niyama thuikā cvana .","वं नियम थुइका च्वन ।"
"1005","He had already apologized before she arrived.","va vaye nhyaḥ he vaṃ māphī phvanedhuṃkūgu khaḥ .","व वये न्ह्यः हे वं माफी फ्वनेधुंकूगु खः ।"
"1006","We have already discussed this matter.","thva khaṃy jimisaṃ sahalaha yāyedhunāgu du .","थ्व खँय् जिमिसं सहलह यायेधुनागु दु ।"
"1007","The message was sent by mistake.","hasanā dvaṃkāḥ chvayāhaḥgu khaḥ .","हसना द्वंकाः छ्वयाहःगु खः ।"
"1008","The speech was heard by thousands.","ugu bhāṣaṇa dvalaṃdvaḥ manūtaysaṃ nyaṃgu khaḥ .","उगु भाषण द्वलंद्वः मनूतय्सं न्यंगु खः ।"
"1009","English is spoken all over the world.","haliṃnyaṃkaṃ aṃgrejī bhāy lhāi .","हलिंन्यंकं अंग्रेजी भाय् ल्हाइ ।"
"1010","The news is being reported now.","āḥ samācāra vayācvaṃgu du .","आः समाचार वयाच्वंगु दु ।"
"1011","Speak clearly.","bāṃlāka khaṃ lhā .","बांलाक खँ ल्हा ।"
"1012","Do not interrupt me.","jitaḥ paṃgaḥ thane mate .","जितः पंगः थने मते ।"
"1013","Listen to what I am saying.","jiṃ dhayācvanāgu khaṃ nyanādisaṃ.","जिं धयाच्वनागु खँ न्‍यनादिसँ।"
"1014","Let us agree to disagree.","asahamata juita sahamata juinu .","असहमत जुइत सहमत जुइनु ।"
"1015","You should listen more carefully.","chiṃ bāṃlāka nyanemāḥ .","छिं बांलाक न्यनेमाः ।"
"1016","I must tell you something important.","jiṃ chanta chuṃ mahatvapūrṇa khaṃ dhāye he māḥ .","जिं छन्त छुं महत्वपूर्ण खँ धाये हे माः ।"
"1017","She might not come to the meeting.","va muṃjyāy mavayephu .","व मुंज्याय् मवयेफु ।"
"1018","He could speak five languages.","vaṃ nyāgū bhāy lhāye saḥ .","वं न्यागू भाय् ल्हाये सः ।"
"1019","If you explain it clearly, everyone will understand.","bāṃlāka thuikā bila dhāḥsā sakasinaṃ thui .","बांलाक थुइका बिल धाःसा सकसिनं थुइ ।"
"1020","If I had known your number, I would have called you.","jiṃ chigu nambara syūgu jūsā, jiṃ chitaḥ phona yāyegu jui .","जिं छिगु नम्बर स्यूगु जूसा, जिं छितः फोन यायेगु जुइ ।"
"1021","If she were here, she would agree.","va thana dugu khaḥsā, va sahamata jui .","व थन दुगु खःसा, व सहमत जुइ ।"
"1022","This language is more difficult than that one.","thva bhāy va bhāy svayāṃ thāku .","थ्व भाय् व भाय् स्वयां थाकु ।"
"1023","He is the most talkative person I know.","jiṃ mhasyūpiṃ madhyay va dakalay khaṃ lhāye yaḥmha manū khaḥ .","जिं म्हस्यूपिं मध्यय् व दकलय् खँ ल्हाये यःम्ह मनू खः ।"
"1024","She speaks more fluently than I do.","vaṃ ji svayāṃ bāṃlāka khaṃ lhāi .","वं जि स्वयां बांलाक खँ ल्हाइ ।"
"1025","The meeting that was held yesterday was productive.","mhigaḥ jūgu muṃjyā utpādanaśīla jula .","म्हिगः जूगु मुँज्या उत्पादनशील जुल ।"
"1026","I met the woman who translated the book.","jiṃ saphū bhāy hilādīmha misāyāta nāpalānā .","जिं सफू भाय् हिलादीम्ह मिसायात नापलाना ।"
"1027","Both speaking and listening are important.","nvavāyegu va nyanegu nittāṃ mahatvapūrṇa khaḥ .","न्ववायेगु व न्यनेगु नित्तां महत्वपूर्ण खः ।"
"1028","Neither she nor I knew the answer.","na vaṃ na jiṃ lisaḥ masyū .","न वं न जिं लिसः मस्यू ।"
"1029","Not only did he apologize, but he also offered to help.","vaṃ kṣamā phvaṃgu jaka makhu, gvāhāli yāyegu prastāva naṃ tala .","वं क्षमा फ्वंगु जक मखु, ग्वाहालि यायेगु प्रस्ताव नं तल ।"
"1030","Whatever you say, I will keep it a secret.","chaṃ nhyāgu he dhāḥsāṃ jiṃ thukiyāta sucukā taye .","छं न्ह्यागु हे धाःसां जिं थुकियात सुचुका तये ।"
"1031","I would rather text than call.","jiṃ kala svayāṃ ṭeksṭa yāyegu bāṃlāi .","जिं कल स्वयां टेक्स्ट यायेगु बांलाइ ।"
"1032","Hardly anyone understood the lecture.","sāyada he sunānaṃ lekcara thuike maphuta .","सायद हे सुनानं लेक्चर थुइके मफुत ।"
"1033","It is said that he speaks seven languages.","vaykalaṃ nhaygū bhāy lhāyesaḥ dhakāḥ dhāi .","वय्कलं न्हय्गू भाय् ल्हायेसः धकाः धाइ ।"
"1034","Everyone deserves to be heard.","sakaleṃ nyanebahaḥpiṃ khaḥ .","सकलें न्यनेबहःपिं खः ।"
"1035","Will you lock the door?","chaṃ lukhāy tālaṃ gvayātai lā ?","छं लुखाय् तालं ग्वयातइ ला ?"
"1036","They used to walk to school.","ipiṃ bvanekuthii nyāsi vanīgu khaḥ .","इपिं ब्वनेकुथिइ न्यासि वनीगु खः ।"
"1037","We had not finished eating when the phone rang.","jimisaṃ naye sidhayeke maphunibalay phona nyāta .","जिमिसं नये सिधयेके मफुनिबलय् फोन न्यात ।"
"1038","The floor is swept every evening.","nhyābaleṃ saniilay baṃ pui .","न्ह्याबलें सनिइलय् बँ पुइ ।"
"1039","Are the children sleeping?","masta dyanācvana lā ?","मस्त द्यनाच्वन ला ?"
"1040","I used to wake up early.","ji nhāpāṃ he danegu yānā .","जि न्हापां हे दनेगु याना ।"
"1041","She will not forget her keys again.","vaṃ hānaṃ vayāgu tāḥcā lvaḥmaṃkī makhu .","वं हानं वयागु ताःचा ल्वःमंकी मखु ।"
"1042","Let the children play outside.","mastay ta pine mhite byu .","मस्तय् त पिने म्हिते ब्यु ।"
"1043","The table is set for dinner.","beli yāyeta ṭebula mile jula .","बेलि यायेत टेबुल मिले जुल ।"
"1044","We were not ready when they came.","ipiṃ vaḥbalay jipiṃ tayāra majūni .","इपिं वःबलय् जिपिं तयार मजूनि ।"
"1045","Whenever it rains, I stay home.","gubaleṃ gubaleṃ vā vai, ji cheṃy he cvane .","गुबलें गुबलें वा वइ, जि छेँय् हे च्वने ।"
"1046","They take turns cleaning the kitchen.","imisaṃ pālaṃpāḥ bhutūkvathā sucukucu yāi .","इमिसं पालंपाः भुतूक्वथा सुचुकुचु याइ ।"
"1047","How often do you brush your hair?","chaṃ chaṃgu saṃ guli yākanaṃ brasa yānā ?","छं छंगु सँ गुलि याकनं ब्रस याना ?"
"1048","My alarm has not gone off yet.","jigu alārma āḥtakaṃ syāḥgu maduni .","जिगु अलार्म आःतकं स्याःगु मदुनि ।"
"1049","They were making dinner when I got home.","ji cheṃy thyaṃbalay imisaṃ beli yānācvaṃgu khaḥ .","जि छेँय् थ्यंबलय् इमिसं बेलि यानाच्वंगु खः ।"
"1050","Will she have finished cooking by then?","vaṃ abalay taka nasā jvare sidhayekī lā ?","वं अबलय् तक नसा ज्वरे सिधयेकी ला ?"
"1051","Do not touch the hot pan.","kvāḥlakhaṃ thiimate .","क्वाःलखं थिइमते ।"
"1052","The bread is baked fresh every morning.","mari nhyābaleṃ suthay tājā beka yāi .","मरि न्ह्याबलें सुथय् ताजा बेक याइ ।"
"1053","You used to eat meat.","chaṃ lā nayegu yānāgu khaḥ .","छं ला नयेगु यानागु खः ।"
"1054","We had not eaten all day.","jimisaṃ nhicchiyaṃkaṃ chuṃ manayā .","जिमिसं न्हिच्छियंकं छुं मनया ।"
"1055","He used to work in a bakery.","vaṃ bekarii jyā yāigu khaḥ .","वं बेकरिइ ज्या याइगु खः ।"
"1056","Are they bringing dessert?","imisaṃ ḍejarṭa hayācvaṃgu khaḥ lā ?","इमिसं डेजर्ट हयाच्वंगु खः ला ?"
"1057","Pass me the butter, please.","jitaḥ baṭara biyādisaṃ .","जितः बटर बियादिसँ ।"
"1058","The vegetables had been washed before cooking.","tarakārī bui nhyaḥ siledhuṃkūgu khaḥ .","तरकारी बुइ न्ह्यः सिलेधुंकूगु खः ।"
"1059","How long have the potatoes been boiling?","ālu kvānācvaṃgu guli data ?","आलु क्वानाच्वंगु गुलि दत ?"
"1060","Let him try a small piece.","vaṃ cikūgu kucā kutaḥ yākebyu .","वं चिकूगु कुचा कुतः याकेब्यु ।"
"1061","Whenever we visit, she makes her special cake.","jipiṃ gubaleṃ naṃ nāpalāyebalay, vaṃ vayāgu viśeṣa keka dayekī .","जिपिं गुबलें नं नापलायेबलय्, वं वयागु विशेष केक दयेकी ।"
"1062","They used to commute by bicycle.","ipiṃ sāikalaṃ vayevane yāigu khaḥ .","इपिं साइकलं वयेवने याइगु खः ।"
"1063","Will you be taking the early train?","chiṃ nhāpāṃ he rela kāyegu lā ?","छिं न्हापां हे रेल कायेगु ला ?"
"1064","I had not been to Europe before that trip.","va yātrā nhyaḥ ji yuropa mavanāni .","व यात्रा न्ह्यः जि युरोप मवनानि ।"
"1065","Let them board first.","imita nhāpāṃ gayeke byu .","इमित न्हापां गयेके ब्यु ।"
"1066","The gate is closed at midnight.","bācā ilay geṭa tinātala .","बाचा इलय् गेट तिनातल ।"
"1067","We were not told about the cancellation.","jimita radda yāḥgu khaṃ madhāḥ .","जिमित रद्द याःगु खँ मधाः ।"
"1068","He used to drive a truck for a living.","vaṃ jīvanayāpanayā niṃtiṃ ṭraka caleyāigu khaḥ .","वं जीवनयापनया निंतिं ट्रक चलेयाइगु खः ।"
"1069","Are you enjoying the flight?","chaṃ phlāiṭayā ānanda kayācvana lā ?","छं फ्लाइटया आनन्द कयाच्वन ला ?"
"1070","Whenever I fly, I get anxious.","ji gubaleṃ gubaleṃ bvaye, ji utsuka jui .","जि गुबलें गुबलें ब्वये, जि उत्सुक जुइ ।"
"1071","How many suitcases may I check?","jiṃ gvaḥgū suṭakesa jāṃca yāye phai ?","जिं ग्वःगू सुटकेस जाँच याये फै ?"
"1072","The tickets have already been purchased.","ṭikaṭa nyāyedhuṃkūgu du .","टिकट न्यायेधुंकूगु दु ।"
"1073","Do not leave your bags unattended.","chaṃgu jholā sunāṃ naṃ masvase tvaḥte mate .","छंगु झोला सुनां नं मस्वसे त्वःते मते ।"
"1074","They used to live next door.","ipiṃ likkasaṃ cvanīgu khaḥ .","इपिं लिक्कसं च्वनीगु खः ।"
"1075","Will you be visiting your parents this weekend?","thugu vāḥyā lipatay chi chimi māṃabuyāta nāpalāyeta jhāyādī lā ?","थुगु वाःया लिपतय् छि छिमि मांअबुयात नापलायेत झायादी ला ?"
"1076","I had not met her husband before the wedding.","ihipā nhyaḥ jiṃ vayā bhāḥtayāta nāpa malānāni .","इहिपा न्ह्यः जिं वया भाःतयात नाप मलानानि ।"
"1077","Let the baby sleep.","macāyāta dyaṃke byu .","मचायात द्यंके ब्यु ।"
"1078","The lawn is mowed once a week.","vālay chakvaḥ khyaḥ pālī .","वालय् छक्वः ख्यः पाली ।"
"1079","We were not expecting guests.","jimisaṃ pāhāṃ vai dhakāḥ manaṃtunāgu makhu .","जिमिसं पाहां वइ धकाः मनंतुनागु मखु ।"
"1080","She used to play with dolls.","va katāṃmari mhitegu yāḥ .","व कतांमरि म्हितेगु याः ।"
"1081","Are your siblings coming for dinner?","chimi dājukijā taḥkeheṃpiṃ beli yāyeta vayācvaṃgu khaḥ lā ?","छिमि दाजुकिजा तःकेहेंपिं बेलि यायेत वयाच्वंगु खः ला ?"
"1082","Whenever grandma visits, she brings cookies.","ajiṃ nāpalāyeta vaibalay, vaṃ kukija jvanā vai .","अजिं नापलायेत वइबलय्, वं कुकिज ज्वना वइ ।"
"1083","How many rooms does the house have?","cheṃy gvaḥkū kvathā du ?","छेँय् ग्वःकू क्वथा दु ?"
"1084","Make yourself at home.","thaḥthamhaṃ cheṃy theṃ dayeki .","थःथम्हं छेँय् थें दयेकि ।"
"1085","The children had already been picked up.","mastay ta lhvane dhuṃkala .","मस्तय् त ल्ह्वने धुंकल ।"
"1086","Do not slam the door.","lukhā tvāthale mate .","लुखा त्वाथले मते ।"
"1087","She used to teach mathematics.","vaṃ gaṇita syanegu yāḥ .","वं गणित स्यनेगु याः ।"

"1200","It took them a whole day to move.","imita saneta nhicchi ī kāla .","इमित सनेत न्हिच्छि ई काल ।"
"1201","She made the children clean their rooms.","vaṃ mastay ta imigu kvathā sucukucu yāke bila .","वं मस्तय् त इमिगु क्वथा सुचुकुचु याके बिल ।"
"1202","My grandmother is the oldest person in our town.","jimi aji jimigu śaharayā dakalay jyāthamha manū khaḥ .","जिमि अजि जिमिगु शहरया दकलय् ज्याथम्ह मनू खः ।"
"1203","Close the window before you leave.","cha vane nhyaḥ jhyāḥ tinābyu .","छ वने न्ह्यः झ्याः तिनाब्यु ।"
"1204","I wonder what time my parents will arrive.","jimi māṃabu guli ilay thyanī dhakāḥ ji ajū cāla .","जिमि मांअबु गुलि इलय् थ्यनी धकाः जि अजू चाल ।"
"1205","There are five people in my family.","jimi cheṃjalay nyāmha du .","जिमि छेँजलय् न्याम्ह दु ।"
"1206","There was a mistake in the calculation.","gaṇanāy galtī jula .","गणनाय् गल्ती जुल ।"
"1207","It takes years to master a skill.","chagū śīpay mahārata hāsila yāyeta daṃ daṃ lage jui .","छगू शीपय् महारत हासिल यायेत दँ दँ लगे जुइ ।"
"1208","She had him rewrite the entire essay.","vaṃ vayāta mukkaṃ cvasu hānaṃ cvayeke bila .","वं वयात मुक्कं च्वसु हानं च्वयेके बिल ।"
"1209","This is the most important exam of the year.","thva dacchiyā dakalay mahatvapūrṇa jāṃca khaḥ .","थ्व दच्छिया दकलय् महत्वपूर्ण जाँच खः ।"
"1210","Finish this before the bell rings.","ghanṭi nyāye nhyaḥ thva jyā sidhayeki .","घन्टि न्याये न्ह्यः थ्व ज्या सिधयेकि ।"
"1211","I wonder whether the results have been posted.","licvaḥ poṣṭa juidhuṃkala lā dhakāḥ ji ajū cāla .","लिच्वः पोष्ट जुइधुंकल ला धकाः जि अजू चाल ।"
"1212","There have been several complaints.","taḥkvaḥmachi gunāso vaḥgu du .","तःक्वःमछि गुनासो वःगु दु ।"
"1213","There are mountains in the distance.","tāpākka guṃcva du .","तापाक्क गुँच्व दु ।"
"1214","It takes sunlight eight minutes to reach Earth.","nibhāḥyā jaḥ pr̥thvīi thyaṃketa cyāgū mineṭa kāi .","निभाःया जः पृथ्वीइ थ्यंकेत च्यागू मिनेट काइ ।"
"1215","The heat made the ice melt.","kvāḥjalaṃ cvāpu nāyābila .","क्वाःजलं च्वापु नायाबिल ।"
"1216","This is the tallest mountain in the country.","thva deyyā dakalay tajāḥgu guṃcva kha.","थ्व देय्‌या दकलय् तजाःगु गुँच्व ख।"
"1217","Watch where you step.","chaṃ gana palāḥ tayā sva .","छं गन पलाः तया स्व ।"
"1218","I wonder if it will rain tonight.","thauṃ cānhay vā vai lā dhakāḥ ji bicāḥ yānā .","थौं चान्हय् वा वइ ला धकाः जि बिचाः याना ।"
"1219","There had been a drought for months.","ana mahinauṃniseṃ khaḍerī jūgu khaḥ .","अन महिनाौंनिसें खडेरी जूगु खः ।"
"1220","There is a pharmacy on the corner.","kunay vāsaḥ pasaḥ du .","कुनय् वासः पसः दु ।"
"1221","It takes six weeks for a bone to heal.","kvaṃy laṃketa khuvāḥ mālī .","क्वँय् लंकेत खुवाः माली ।"
"1222","The noise made my headache worse.","hallāyā huniṃ jigu chyaṃ syāta .","हल्लाया हुनिं जिगु छ्यं स्यात ।"
"1223","This is the best hospital in the city.","thva śaharayā dakalay bāṃlāḥgu aspatāla khaḥ .","थ्व शहरया दकलय् बांलाःगु अस्पताल खः ।"
"1224","Breathe deeply and count to ten.","tāḥhākaḥ sāsaḥ lhā ale jhigū taka lyāḥ khā .","ताःहाकः सासः ल्हा अले झिगू तक ल्याः खा ।"
"1225","I wonder if the results are normal.","licvaḥ sāmānya khaḥ lā dhakāḥ jiṃ bicāḥ yānā .","लिच्वः सामान्य खः ला धकाः जिं बिचाः याना ।"
"1226","There have been no cases of infection.","koronā saṃkramaṇayā ghaṭanā dhāḥsā khanemaduni .","कोरोना संक्रमणया घटना धाःसा खनेमदुनि ।"
"1227","There is a discount on bulk orders.","balka arḍaray chyāpaṃ du .","बल्क अर्डरय् छ्यापं दु ।"
"1228","It took me an hour to find the right size.","pāy chigu sāija luiketa jitaḥ chaghau ī kāla .","पाय् छिगु साइज लुइकेत जितः छघौ ई काल ।"
"1229","The advertisement made me want to buy it.","vijñāpanaṃ jitaḥ thva nyāyegu icchā yāta .","विज्ञापनं जितः थ्व न्यायेगु इच्छा यात ।"
"1230","This is the cheapest option available.","thva dakalay daṃgu vikalpa khaḥ .","थ्व दकलय् दंगु विकल्प खः ।"
"1231","Compare prices before you decide.","chiṃ kvaḥchī nhyaḥ mū dānādisaṃ .","छिं क्वःछी न्ह्यः मू दानादिसँ ।"
"1232","I wonder whether they accept credit cards.","imisaṃ kreḍiṭa kārḍa svīkāra yāi lā dhakāḥ ji ajū cāla .","इमिसं क्रेडिट कार्ड स्वीकार याइ ला धकाः जि अजू चाल ।"
"1233","There were long lines at every checkout.","cekaāuṭa pattikaṃ tāḥhākaḥgu jhvaḥ dugu khaḥ .","चेकआउट पत्तिकं ताःहाकःगु झ्वः दुगु खः ।"
"1234","There is a bond between them.","imi dathui chagū bandhana du .","इमि दथुइ छगू बन्धन दु ।"

"1236","Her words made me cry.","vayā khaṃgvalaṃ jitaḥ khvayekala .","वया खँग्वलं जितः ख्वयेकल ।"
"1237","This is the saddest story I have ever heard.","thva jiṃ āḥtaka nyanāgu bākhaṃ madhyay dakalay duḥkhada bākhaṃ khaḥ .","थ्व जिं आःतक न्यनागु बाखं मध्यय् दकलय् दुःखद बाखं खः ।"
"1238","Be honest with yourself.","thaḥthamhaṃ imāndāra ju .","थःथम्हं इमान्दार जु ।"

"1240","There had been tension between them for weeks.","imi dathui chuṃ vāḥniseṃ tanāva juyācvaṃgu khaḥ .","इमि दथुइ छुं वाःनिसें तनाव जुयाच्वंगु खः ।"

"1246","I wonder what time the show starts.","śo guli ilay nhyāi dhakāḥ ji ajū cāla .","शो गुलि इलय् न्ह्याइ धकाः जि अजू चाल ।"
"1247","There have been three sequels so far.","āḥtaka svaṃgū sikvela pihāṃ vaḥgu du .","आःतक स्वंगू सिक्वेल पिहां वःगु दु ।"
"1248","There is a misunderstanding between us.","jhī dathui galataphahamī juyācvaṃgu du .","झी दथुइ गलतफहमी जुयाच्वंगु दु ।"
"1249","It took her courage to speak up.","nvavāyeta vayāta sāhasa māḥgu khaḥ .","न्ववायेत वयात साहस माःगु खः ।"
"1250","His tone made everyone uncomfortable.","vayāgu salaṃ sakasitaṃ asahaja yāta .","वयागु सलं सकसितं असहज यात ।"
"1251","This is the most important conversation we have had.","thva jimigu khaṃlhābalhā madhyay dakalay mahatvapūrṇa khaḥ .","थ्व जिमिगु खँल्हाबल्हा मध्यय् दकलय् महत्वपूर्ण खः ।"
"1252","Repeat after me.","jigu liuliu dohare yā .","जिगु लिउलिउ दोहरे या ।"
"1253","I wonder why they have not replied.","imisaṃ chāy lisaḥ mabyū dhakāḥ ji ajū cāla .","इमिसं छाय् लिसः मब्यू धकाः जि अजू चाल ।"
"1254","There used to be more communication between neighbors.","nhāpā nhāpā jaḥlākhaḥlātay dathui apvaḥ khaṃlhābalhā juigu khaḥ .","न्हापा न्हापा जःलाखःलातय् दथुइ अप्वः खँल्हाबल्हा जुइगु खः ।"
"1255","You forgot to turn off the stove.","chaṃ sṭobha syāye lvaḥmana .","छं स्टोभ स्याये ल्वःमन ।"
"1256","You were reading when the power went out.","bijuli syāḥbalay chaṃ bvanācvana .","बिजुलि स्याःबलय् छं ब्वनाच्वन ।"
"1257","You had already left when I arrived.","ji thyaṃbalay cha vanedhuṃkūgu khaḥ .","जि थ्यंबलय् छ वनेधुंकूगु खः ।"
"1258","You burned the toast again.","chaṃ hānaṃ ṭosṭa cyākala .","छं हानं टोस्ट च्याकल ।"
"1259","You were chopping vegetables when I called.","jiṃ phona yānābalay chaṃ tarakārī kucā yānācvana .","जिं फोन यानाबलय् छं तरकारी कुचा यानाच्वन ।"
"1260","You missed the last bus.","chaṃ lipāṃgu basa tvaḥphikala .","छं लिपांगु बस त्वःफिकल ।"
"1261","You were packing when I arrived.","ji thyaṃbalay chaṃ sāmāna pvaḥcinā cvana .","जि थ्यंबलय् छं सामान प्वःचिना च्वन ।"
"1262","You were born in a small town.","cha cidhaṃgu śaharay būgu khaḥ .","छ चिधंगु शहरय् बूगु खः ।"
"1263","You grew up near the ocean.","cha samudrayā likka bvalaṃgu khaḥ .","छ समुद्रया लिक्क ब्वलंगु खः ।"
"1264","You forgot your homework at home.","chaṃ chaṃgu homavarka cheṃy lvaḥmaṃkala .","छं छंगु होमवर्क छेँय् ल्वःमंकल ।"
"1265","You were not paying attention.","chaṃ dhyāna biyācvaṃgu madu .","छं ध्यान बियाच्वंगु मदु ।"
"1266","You saw a shooting star last night.","chaṃ mhigaḥ cānhay suṭiṅga sṭāra khanā .","छं म्हिगः चान्हय् सुटिङ्ग स्टार खना ।"
"1267","You were hiking when the storm began.","vāphay vaḥbalay cha hāikiṅga yānācvanāgu khaḥ .","वाफय् वःबलय् छ हाइकिङ्ग यानाच्वनागु खः ।"

"1270","You lost your receipt.","chaṃ chaṃgu rasida taṃkala .","छं छंगु रसिद तंकल ।"
"1271","You were browsing when the store closed.","pasaḥ banda juibalay chaṃ brāuja yānācvanāgu khaḥ .","पसः बन्द जुइबलय् छं ब्राउज यानाच्वनागु खः ।"
"1272","You cried at the ending.","antay cha khvala .","अन्तय् छ ख्वल ।"
"1273","You were not listening to me.","chaṃ jigu khaṃ nyanācvaṃgu madu .","छं जिगु खँ न्यनाच्वंगु मदु ।"
"1274","You won the tournament.","chaṃ dheṃ dheṃ ballāḥ tyākala .","छं धें धें बल्लाः त्याकल ।"
"1275","You were singing along to the music.","chaṃ saṃgītayā liseṃ me hālācvaṃgu khaḥ .","छं संगीतया लिसें मे हालाच्वंगु खः ।"
"1276","You interrupted me.","chaṃ jitaḥ paṃgaḥ thana .","छं जितः पंगः थन ।"
"1277","You were whispering during the speech.","nvavāyegu jhvalay chaṃ khiuṃse cvanāḥ khaṃ lhānācvana .","न्ववायेगु झ्वलय् छं खिउँसे च्वनाः खँ ल्हानाच्वन ।"

"1279","They will clean the garage tomorrow.","imisaṃ kanhay gyāreja sucukucu yāi .","इमिसं कन्हय् ग्यारेज सुचुकुचु याइ ।"
"1280","We will try a new restaurant tonight.","jimisaṃ thauṃ bahanī nhūgu reṣṭurāṃ svaye .","जिमिसं थौं बहनी न्हूगु रेष्टुराँ स्वये ।"

"1283","They will not arrive until midnight.","ipiṃ bācā cānhay taka thyanī makhu .","इपिं बाचा चान्हय् तक थ्यनी मखु ।"
"1284","We will move to a bigger house next year.","jipiṃ megu daṃy ta:kūgu cheṃy vane .","जिपिं मेगु दँय् त:कूगु छेँय् वने ।"

"1287","It will snow tonight.","thauṃ cānhay cvāpu gāi .","थौं चान्हय् च्वापु गाइ ।"

"1289","I will see the dentist on Monday.","jiṃ somabārakhunhu danta cikitsakayāta svaye .","जिं सोमबारखुन्हु दन्त चिकित्सकयात स्वये ।"

"1299","They cleaned the entire house yesterday.","imisaṃ mhigaḥ cheṃ dakvaṃ sucukucu yāta .","इमिसं म्हिगः छेँ दक्वं सुचुकुचु यात ।"

"1302","Is the coffee being brewed?","kaphī dayekā cvana lā ?","कफी दयेका च्वन ला ?"
"1303","All windows must be shut before you leave.","cha vane nhyaḥ dakvaṃ jhyāḥ tinā taye he māḥ .","छ वने न्ह्यः दक्वं झ्याः तिना तये हे माः ।"
"1304","By this time next year, we will have moved.","megu daṃyā thugu ilay taka, jipiṃ sare juidhuṃkūgu jui .","मेगु दँया थुगु इलय् तक, जिपिं सरे जुइधुंकूगु जुइ ।"
"1305","They did not hear the alarm.","imisaṃ alārma matāḥ .","इमिसं अलार्म मताः ।"
"1306","Has she finished getting dressed?","vaṃ vasaḥ pune sidhala lā ?","वं वसः पुने सिधल ला ?"

"1599","They were arguing over the bill.","ipiṃ vidheyakayāta kayāḥ vivāda yānācvaṃgu khaḥ .","इपिं विधेयकयात कयाः विवाद यानाच्वंगु खः ।"
"1600","This brand is cheaper than that one.","thva brāṇḍa va svayāṃ daṃ .","थ्व ब्राण्ड व स्वयां दं ।"

"1695","You have not finished your plate.","chaṃ chaṃgu pleṭa sidhayeke maphuni .","छं छंगु प्लेट सिधयेके मफुनि ।"

"1698","You will enjoy the view from the top.","chaṃ cvay niseṃ khanedaigu dr̥śyayā ānanda kāyekhanī .","छं च्वय् निसें खनेदइगु दृश्यया आनन्द कायेखनी ।"

"1700","You used to travel a lot for work.","cha jyāyā niṃtiṃ yakva cāḥhilegu yānā .","छ ज्याया निंतिं यक्व चाःहिलेगु याना ।"
"1701","I am not used to flying.","ji bvayegu bāni madu .","जि ब्वयेगु बानि मदु ।"

"1710","You used to fight with your sister a lot.","cha chimi keheṃlise yakva lvāpu juigu khaḥ .","छ छिमि केहेंलिसे यक्व ल्वापु जुइगु खः ।"
